The elite frequently bypass hunting laws, organizing illegal safaris that decimate
endangered species, while local poachers operate under the protection of corrupt
politicians. Takweyat will aggressively prosecute both the poachers and the state officials
who grant them illegal permits. We will legally mandate the uncompromising expansion of
protected wildlife corridors, stripping the licenses of any corporate or agricultural entity
that encroaches on these essential natural habitats.
21.5. Holding Manufacturers Liable for Single-Use Plastics
The burden of plastic pollution has unfairly been placed on the consumer, while the
corporations manufacturing billions of non-biodegradable wrappers reap massive profits.
We will draft and enforce "Extended Producer Responsibility" EPR legislation. We will
legally force multinational food and beverage corporations to physically retrieve, recycle,
and pay for the disposal of their plastic packaging, suing them for the environmental
devastation caused by their products.
21.6. Fighting Urban Deforestation and Real Estate Expansion
Real estate mafias continually bribe city planners to rezone protected urban greenbelts
and historical parks into luxury housing or commercial plazas, drastically increasing city
temperatures. We will file high-court stays the moment a bulldozer touches protected
urban greenery. Takweyat will sue municipal authorities for corruption, legally enforcing a
mandatory ratio of green space per capita that no state or private entity is permitted to
alter or develop.
21.7. Soil Degradation and Agrochemical Liability
Agrochemical monopolies have convinced farmers to saturate their lands with chemical
fertilizers and pesticides, which temporarily boost yields but permanently destroy the
soil's microbiome and poison the food supply. We will file class-action lawsuits against

these chemical giants for ecological sabotage. We will legally force the state to subsidize
massive organic transition programs, compensating farmers whose ancestral lands have
been rendered barren by these toxic chemicals.
21.8. Regulating Noise and Light Pollution
Noise and light pollution are rarely treated as legal issues, yet they cause severe
psychological distress, sleep deprivation, and disruption to local wildlife. We will draft
strict municipal legislation capping decibel levels for factories, traffic, and public events,
alongside mandating dark-sky compliance for commercial lighting. Takweyat will sue
entities that violate these limits, establishing that a peaceful, quiet environment is a
fundamental public health right.
21.9. Empowering and Shielding Local Climate Wardens
Local environmental activists and indigenous forest guards are frequently threatened,
beaten, or assassinated by the timber and mining mafias they attempt to expose. We will
establish a specialized, heavily armed legal defense unit explicitly for the protection of
climate activists. We will elevate any threat against an environmental warden to a federal
terrorism charge, utilizing the full weight of the supreme courts to dismantle the mafias
targeting them.
21.10. Strict Accountability for Fossil Fuel Subsidies
Governments routinely utilize taxpayer money to subsidize the coal and oil industries,
artificially inflating the profitability of systems that are destroying the planet. Takweyat will
file constitutional challenges against the state for misusing public funds to finance
ecological destruction. We will legally compel the government to redirect all fossil fuel
subsidies immediately and entirely into the development of decentralized,
community-owned renewable energy grids.
22. Privacy Rights, Digital Autonomy & Anti-Surveillance
22.1. Striking Down Mass Biometric Surveillance
Governments and private corporations are rapidly installing facial recognition cameras in
public spaces, tracking the movements of citizens without warrants or consent. Takweyat
will fiercely challenge the legality of mass biometric surveillance. We will argue before the
supreme courts that recording a citizen's face without explicit suspicion of a crime is an
unconstitutional violation of privacy, legally forcing the dismantling of these Orwellian
surveillance networks.

22.2. Protection of Genomic and Medical Data
With the rise of digital health records and commercial DNA testing, highly sensitive
genetic and medical information is being harvested, bought, and sold by insurance
companies and tech giants. We will draft ironclad legislation classifying genomic data as
sovereign, inalienable property. Takweyat will file massive lawsuits against any entity that
shares, sells, or exposes patient medical records, prosecuting executives for severe
medical privacy violations.
22.3. Banning Unauthorized Geo-Tracking and Location Harvesting
Telecommunication companies and app developers constantly track the real-time GPS
locations of users, selling this data to marketing firms, private investigators, and state
agencies. We will legally mandate that location tracking must be strictly opt-in and
temporary. We will sue telecom monopolies that fail to secure location data, establishing
legal precedents that unauthorized geo-tracking is a form of digital stalking and a severe
criminal offense.
22.4. The Absolute Right to Digital Anonymity
Authoritarian regimes are attempting to legally mandate that every social media account
be tied to a government-issued ID, effectively destroying the ability of whistleblowers and
activists to speak out safely. Takweyat will relentlessly fight these digital identity
mandates in court. We will legally defend the fundamental right to anonymity on the
internet, arguing that forced identification is a direct, calculated assault on the freedom of
speech and political dissent.
22.5. Criminalizing State Spyware Deployment
Governments utilize military-grade spyware (like Pegasus) to hack the phones of
journalists, opposition politicians, and human rights lawyers, turning their personal
devices into state listening posts. We will file high-treason lawsuits against intelligence
officials who authorize the deployment of spyware against domestic civilians. We will
legally force the state to disclose all contracts with foreign cyber-espionage firms,
pushing for total bans on such software.
22.6. Financial Privacy and the War on Cash
The aggressive push toward completely cashless societies allows the state and banks to
monitor, freeze, or heavily tax every single transaction a citizen makes, destroying

financial autonomy. We will legally challenge policies designed to eliminate physical
currency. Takweyat will defend the constitutional right of citizens to transact anonymously
using cash, preventing the government from utilizing digital banking as a tool for total
algorithmic control.
22.7. Drone Surveillance and Airspace Privacy
The unregulated use of drones by police, media, and private corporations is destroying
the concept of privacy within one's own home and property. We will draft comprehensive
drone privacy laws that establish a strict "vertical privacy column" above private
residences. Takweyat will legally empower citizens to disable unauthorized drones
hovering over their property, and we will sue drone operators for criminal trespassing and
unlawful surveillance.
22.8. Regulating IoT Internet of Things) Spying
Smart TVs, virtual assistants, and internet-connected home appliances constantly record
audio and user habits inside the home, transmitting this intimate data back to corporate
servers. We will launch class-action lawsuits against tech manufacturers for designing
appliances that act as corporate wiretaps. We will legally force companies to implement
physical kill-switches for microphones and cameras on all smart devices sold within the
country.
22.9. Privacy by Design in Software Development
Software is often deliberately designed to extract maximum data, forcing users to
navigate complex, deceptive menus to turn off tracking. Takweyat will legally mandate the
principle of "Privacy by Design." We will force tech developers to ensure that the absolute
highest level of privacy and data security is the default setting on every application,
heavily fining companies that utilize deceptive UI practices (dark patterns) to trick users
into giving up data.
22.10. Workplace Surveillance and Employee Privacy
Corporations increasingly monitor their employees' keystrokes, emails, and even eye
movements via webcams during remote work, creating a deeply dystopian and stressful
work environment. We will draft and enforce legislation that completely bans continuous,
algorithmic surveillance of employees. Takweyat will sue corporations that utilize these
digital sweatshop tactics, legally enshrining the right of workers to maintain their dignity
and privacy during working hours.

23. Corporate Manslaughter, Industrial Negligence & Executive Liability
23.1. Piercing the Corporate Veil for Fatal Negligence
When a factory burns down or a mine collapses due to bypassed safety protocols, the
corporation simply pays a fine while the executives responsible remain untouched.
Takweyat will shatter the legal fiction that shields executives. We will aggressively pursue
"Corporate Manslaughter" charges, holding CEOs, board members, and safety inspectors
personally, criminally, and financially liable for the deaths of their workers, ensuring they
face decades in prison.
23.2. The Construction Mafia and Building Collapses
Corrupt real estate developers routinely bribe building inspectors to use substandard
steel, cheap cement, and faulty foundations to maximize profits, resulting in catastrophic
building collapses that kill hundreds. We will treat these collapses as acts of mass murder.
Takweyat will legally freeze all assets of the developers, prosecuting them alongside the
state engineers who approved the blueprints, ensuring victims' families receive
generational compensation.
23.3. Chemical Spills and Industrial Disasters
Industrial plants frequently operate highly toxic processes in the middle of densely
populated urban areas, with zero emergency containment systems. When chemical leaks
cause mass casualties or long-term health crises, we will launch devastating international
and domestic litigation. We will legally force these industries to relocate far from civilian
centers, mandating that they pay the lifetime medical bills for every individual exposed to
their toxic negligence.
23.4. Transit Negligence and Mass Casualties
Transport monopolies operate fleets of dangerously obsolete buses, trains, and ferries,
forcing exhausted drivers to work back-to-back shifts that inevitably result in fatal
accidents. We will refuse to accept these events as "accidents." Takweyat will file
massive civil and criminal suits against the transport owners, legally forcing them to
liquidate their fleets to pay astronomical death compensations, thereby making it
financially impossible to operate unsafe transit networks.
23.5. Defective Consumer Products Resulting in Death
Corporations frequently calculate that paying out a few wrongful death lawsuits is
cheaper than recalling millions of defective vehicles, faulty electrical appliances, or toxic

toys. We will brutally upend this corporate calculus. Takweyat will file lawsuits demanding
punitive damages so exceptionally high that they threaten the corporation's survival,
legally forcing them to implement immediate, global recalls the moment a fatal defect is
discovered.
23.6. Eradicating NDAs in Cases of Wrongful Death
When corporate negligence kills someone, the company quickly offers the grieving family
a financial settlement on the strict condition that they sign a Non-Disclosure Agreement
NDA, hiding the deadly hazard from the public. We will draft legislation rendering all
NDAs strictly illegal and unenforceable in cases involving physical injury or death. We will
ensure that the truth behind corporate negligence is permanently exposed in open court.
23.7. Fire Safety Evasion in Sweatshops
Garment factories and sweatshops routinely lock emergency exits, block stairwells with
inventory, and refuse to install sprinkler systems, turning their buildings into death traps
during fires. We will establish independent fire-safety audit teams. Takweyat will
proactively sue factory owners who violate fire codes, and in the event of a fatal fire, we
will prosecute the international brands that sourced from the factory for extreme
complicity in the deaths.
23.8. Independent Tribunals for Corporate Homicide
Local labor courts and police are easily bought off by wealthy industrialists, leading to the
rapid burial of evidence after an industrial disaster. Takweyat will aggressively advocate
for the establishment of specialized, fiercely independent tribunals dedicated solely to
industrial accidents and corporate manslaughter. These tribunals will possess the
absolute legal authority to bypass local police and independently seize corporate servers
and internal communications.
23.9. Prosecuting the Suppression of Safety Whistleblowers
Executives often fire or threaten engineers and safety officers who raise alarms about
dangerous machinery or structural flaws before a disaster happens. We will provide an
impenetrable legal shield for safety whistleblowers. If a disaster occurs after a
whistleblower was silenced, Takweyat will use this suppression as absolute legal proof of
premeditated corporate negligence, guaranteeing maximum criminal sentences for the
involved executives.

23.10. Massive Punitive Restitution for Victims' Families
The current legal system places a disgustingly low monetary value on the life of a laborer,
often offering families a paltry sum after a fatal accident. We will legally redefine the
calculation of wrongful death compensation. Takweyat will demand payouts that cover
the lost generational wealth, psychological trauma, and complete educational funding for
the victim's children, ensuring the corporation is financially crippled by their failure to
protect human life.
24. Healthcare Equity, Medical Security & Universal Access
24.1. Abolishing the Two-Tier Healthcare System
The ruling elite and policymakers deliberately neglect public hospitals because they have
exclusive access to elite private clinics or fly abroad for treatment. Takweyat will file
unprecedented constitutional petitions mandating that all elected officials and top-tier
bureaucrats are legally barred from seeking medical treatment outside the domestic
public healthcare system. By forcing the elite to rely on state hospitals, we will guarantee
an immediate, massive upgrade in public medical facilities.
24.2. Legal Right to Free Lifesaving Medications
Essential, life-saving drugs like insulin, asthma inhalers, and basic oncology medications
are frequently priced out of reach for the poor, effectively condemning them to death. We
will fiercely litigate to legally classify these essential medicines as basic human rights, not
commercial commodities. Takweyat will force the state to cap prices severely and
subsidize the local manufacturing of these drugs, breaking the stranglehold of
multinational pharmaceutical cartels.
24.3. Decriminalization of Medical Debt
Families are frequently forced to sell their homes or face imprisonment because they
cannot pay the exorbitant bills generated by emergency medical care in private hospitals.
We will draft legislation that completely decriminalizes medical debt. Takweyat will legally
ban hospitals from holding patients hostage, withholding dead bodies, or seizing primary
residences to recover medical expenses, shifting the financial burden back to state
healthcare insurance schemes.
24.4. Banning Corporate Monopolies on Medical Equipment
Multinational corporations manufacture critical hospital equipment (like MRI machines and
ventilators) using proprietary software, refusing to allow local engineers to repair them,

resulting in broken equipment sitting idle in public hospitals. We will legally challenge
these repair monopolies. We will enforce "Right to Repair" laws for medical devices,
forcing corporations to open-source their repair manuals and allow affordable, local
maintenance of lifesaving machines.
24.5. Combating Medical Racism, Sexism, and Casteism
Marginalized communities, women, and minorities frequently receive lower quality of
care, have their pain dismissed, or are subjected to non-consensual procedures by biased
medical staff. We will establish a legal watchdog to monitor discrimination in healthcare
delivery. Takweyat will file massive civil rights lawsuits against hospitals and medical
professionals who demonstrate systemic bias, legally enforcing mandatory, rigorous
anti-bias training across all medical institutions.
24.6. Maternal Healthcare as an Unconditional Right
The high rate of maternal and infant mortality in rural areas is a direct result of state
negligence and a lack of basic obstetric infrastructure. We will file public interest
litigations holding the state health ministry directly accountable for maternal deaths.
Takweyat will legally compel the government to establish fully equipped, free maternity
clinics within a 10-kilometer radius of every rural settlement, staffed by highly trained,
well-compensated female healthcare workers.
24.7. Transparency in Pharmaceutical Clinical Trials
Western pharmaceutical companies frequently outsource their clinical trials to developing
nations, testing experimental drugs on impoverished, uninformed populations without
proper consent or follow-up care. We will legally mandate the absolute transparency of all
clinical trials. Takweyat will sue pharmaceutical giants that exploit the poor as test
subjects, legally forcing them to provide lifetime medical care and massive financial
compensation to anyone injured during these trials.
24.8. Banning the Privatization and Profiteering of Blood Banks
Blood is freely donated by citizens during crises, yet private hospitals and corrupt blood
banks hoard and sell it at extortionate prices to desperate families. We will file aggressive
legal actions to criminalize the commercial sale of human blood. Takweyat will legally
mandate that all blood banks operate on a strictly non-profit, state-regulated basis,
prosecuting any entity caught profiteering off the life-saving donations of the public.

24.9. Enforcing Minimum Ratios of Rural Healthcare Facilities
The vast majority of doctors are concentrated in wealthy urban centers, leaving rural
populations entirely without access to modern medicine. We will draft legislation legally
mandating a fair geographic distribution of medical resources. We will force the state to
incentivize rural postings heavily, and legally challenge the licenses of private medical
colleges that do not require their graduates to serve a mandatory, paid residency in
underserved rural communities.
24.10. Access to Holistic and Traditional Medicine
Multinational pharmaceutical companies often lobby to legally ban or suppress highly
effective, affordable traditional and indigenous medical practices to eliminate competition.
We will provide legal defense for certified practitioners of traditional and holistic
medicine. Takweyat will ensure that citizens maintain the legal right to choose alternative
therapies, legally integrating proven traditional practices into the national healthcare
framework alongside modern allopathy.
25. Financial De-Monopolization, Economic Democracy & Fair Markets
25.1. Shattering Corporate Cartels and Price-Fixing Syndicates
The national economy is frequently held hostage by cement, sugar, and wheat cartels that
artificially create shortages to manipulate prices, starving the poor while reaping billions.
Takweyat will bypass weak state regulatory bodies and file direct criminal anti-trust
lawsuits against these syndicates. We will aggressively prosecute the cartel leaders for
economic terrorism, legally forcing the breakup of these monopolies into smaller,
intensely competitive entities.
25.2. Auditing and Restructuring the National Debt
The nation's crushing external debt was largely accrued by corrupt regimes, spent on
elite projects, and immediately stolen, leaving the working class to pay it back through
crippling inflation. We will establish a coalition of international financial lawyers to
conduct a forensic audit of the national debt. Takweyat will utilize the legal doctrine of
"Odious Debt" to refuse repayment on loans that were clearly stolen or used to oppress
the public, challenging global creditors in international courts.
25.3. Banning Elite Tax Amnesties and Loopholes
Governments routinely announce "tax amnesty" schemes that allow billionaires, corrupt
politicians, and real estate mafias to whitewash their black money by paying a fraction of

what they owe. We will file supreme court petitions declaring all such amnesties
inherently unconstitutional and discriminatory against honest taxpayers. Takweyat will
legally force the state to retroactively prosecute tax evaders, seizing their assets without
mercy or negotiation.
25.4. Democratization of Central Banking and Monetary Policy
Central banks operate in complete secrecy, often setting interest rates and monetary
policies that protect the banking elite while destroying the purchasing power of the
working class. We will legally challenge the unchecked autonomy of central banks.
Takweyat will draft legislation mandating extreme transparency in monetary policy
decisions, legally requiring the inclusion of labor union leaders and civil society experts
on central banking boards.
25.5. Capping Wealth Inequality and Mandating Profit Sharing
Corporations post record-breaking profits while their workers rely on food charities to
survive. We will draft radical economic legislation legally capping the ratio between CEO
compensation and the lowest-paid worker's salary. Takweyat will aggressively push for
laws that mandate mandatory profit-sharing, ensuring that a legally defined percentage of
all corporate profits must be distributed directly to the employees who generated that
wealth.
25.6. Banning Predatory Speculation on Essential Commodities
Wall Street and local financial elites gamble on the future prices of essential human
needs—like wheat, rice, and housing—artificially driving up costs and causing real-world
famines and homelessness. We will launch aggressive legal campaigns to ban the trading
of basic food staples and primary residential real estate on speculative futures markets.
We will legally separate human survival from casino capitalism.
25.7. Expanding Access to Zero-Interest Micro-Grants
The traditional micro-finance model is highly predatory, trapping the poor in cycles of
high-interest debt that often leads to generational poverty. Rooted in Islamic economic
principles, Takweyat will establish a robust legal framework to replace micro-loans with
decentralized, zero-interest micro-grants Qard al-Hasan). We will legally force major
commercial banks to allocate a percentage of their massive profits to fund these
zero-interest community trusts.

25.8. Breaking the Banking Oligopoly and Promoting Credit Unions
A handful of massive commercial banks control the entire financial system, charging
exorbitant fees and refusing to lend to small businesses or poor farmers. We will file
anti-trust litigations to break up these "too-big-to-fail" institutions. Takweyat will draft
legislation heavily subsidizing the creation of localized, member-owned credit unions and
cooperative banks, democratizing access to capital for the working class.
25.9. Transparency in Foreign Direct Investment FDI
Foreign governments and corporations buy up vast tracts of national land, critical
infrastructure, and mineral rights in opaque deals signed by corrupt officials. We will file
public interest litigations demanding absolute transparency in all FDI contracts. Takweyat
will legally mandate that all foreign investments must include strict technology transfer
clauses and guarantee that the majority of the wealth generated remains within the host
country.
25.10. Reclaiming Privatized State Assets
Vital national assets—such as telecommunications, national airlines, and energy
grids—were often sold to corporate cronies at a fraction of their value under the guise of
"privatization." We will launch forensic legal audits into the history of these sales. Where
corruption or severe undervaluation is proven, Takweyat will file supreme court petitions
to legally void the contracts, reclaiming these strategic assets for the state without
compensating the corrupt buyers.
26. Consumer Rights, Product Safety & Market Integrity
26.1. Criminalizing Deceptive Marketing and Bait-and-Switch Tactics
Corporations routinely manipulate the public with false advertising, burying hidden fees,
and utilizing bait-and-switch pricing models to extort money from unsuspecting
consumers. Takweyat will file massive class-action lawsuits against companies that
intentionally mislead the public. We will legally force these corporations to run highly
visible, corrective public advertising campaigns admitting their fraud, alongside paying
exorbitant punitive damages directly to the exploited consumer base.
26.2. Enforcing the Absolute "Right to Repair"
Tech and automotive monopolies deliberately design their products to be unrepairable by
independent mechanics, forcing consumers to pay extortionate fees at official service
centers. We will aggressively draft and lobby for national "Right to Repair" legislation.

Takweyat will sue manufacturers who utilize proprietary screws, software locks, or
warranty voids to prevent independent repairs, legally mandating that all diagnostic tools
and spare parts be made publicly available at fair market prices.
26.3. Banning Predatory Subscriptions and "Dark Patterns"
Digital corporations utilize manipulative user interfaces, known as "dark patterns," to trick
consumers into signing up for expensive, recurring subscriptions that are deliberately
engineered to be nearly impossible to cancel. We will launch devastating legal attacks on
tech companies employing these deceptive practices. We will legally mandate that
canceling a subscription must take exactly the same number of clicks as signing up,
prosecuting executives for digital extortion.
26.4. Strict Liability for Hazardous and Counterfeit Goods
The markets of the developing world are flooded with counterfeit electronics, toxic toys,
and substandard cosmetics that bypass safety regulations and cause severe injuries or
long-term health crises. Takweyat will legally pierce the corporate veil of e-commerce
platforms and local distributors that allow these goods into the market. We will hold the
platforms criminally and financially liable for every injury caused, forcing them to
implement military-grade vetting of their supply chains.
26.5. Eliminating Hidden Banking Fees and Overdraft Traps
Commercial banks systematically drain the accounts of the working class through hidden
maintenance fees, arbitrary penalty charges, and deliberately complex overdraft traps.
We will file sweeping public interest litigations challenging the legality of these unearned
corporate cash grabs. Takweyat will legally force the central banking authorities to cap all
transactional fees and mandate that banks immediately refund billions in illegally
extracted hidden charges to the public.
26.6. Transparency in Supply Chain Ethics and Sourcing
Consumers have the fundamental right to know if the products they purchase are
manufactured using child labor, environmental destruction, or funding conflict zones. We
will draft legislation that makes strict, verifiable supply chain transparency legally
mandatory. We will sue corporations that falsely claim "ethical sourcing," utilizing
international trade laws to heavily fine and publicly expose brands that rely on modern
slavery or ecological devastation.

26.7. Punishing Corporate Price Gouging During National Crises
During floods, pandemics, or economic crashes, corporate cartels and local distributors
deliberately hoard essential goods—such as face masks, generators, and staple
foods—to artificially inflate prices. We will push for anti-trust laws that categorize crisis
profiteering as an act of economic terrorism. Takweyat will deploy legal teams to force the
state to raid hoarding facilities, confiscate the goods without compensation, and
criminally prosecute the cartel leaders.
26.8. Outlawing the Practice of Planned Obsolescence
Appliance and electronics manufacturers intentionally build products with cheap
components designed to fail shortly after the warranty expires, creating massive
electronic waste and forcing constant repurchasing. We will file massive civil suits against
manufacturers for this practice of economic sabotage. We will legally force them to
provide hardware warranties for a minimum of a decade, reducing environmental waste
and protecting the financial stability of the working class.
26.9. Protecting Consumers from Aggressive Debt Collection
Collection agencies often utilize mafia-style intimidation tactics, harassing consumers at
their workplaces, threatening their families, and driving individuals to severe depression
or suicide. Takweyat will launch an all-out legal assault on predatory collection practices.
We will file injunctions to criminalize abusive debt collection, suing these agencies for
severe psychological distress and legally revoking the operating licenses of banks that
employ them.
26.10. Establishing Independent, Fast-Track Consumer Tribunals
The traditional court system is too slow and expensive for a consumer seeking justice
over a faulty refrigerator or a scammed airline ticket, allowing corporations to simply wait
out the plaintiff. We will aggressively advocate for the creation of completely
independent, fast-track consumer tribunals. These courts will operate digitally, require no
lawyers for the plaintiff, and possess the absolute legal authority to issue immediate,
binding financial judgments against corporations.
27. Urban Justice, Housing Equity & Civic Infrastructure
27.1. Eradicating Slum Clearances and Forced Evictions
Municipal governments and elite real estate developers routinely bulldoze generational
slums without warning, violently displacing the urban poor to build luxury commercial

plazas. Takweyat will provide fierce, immediate legal defense to communities facing
eviction. We will file high-court stays to halt illegal demolitions, prosecuting state officials
who authorize the destruction of homes without providing prime, equivalent resettlement
and comprehensive financial compensation.
27.2. Rent Control and Anti-Gentrification Legislation
Unregulated landlords and speculative real estate investors continuously drive up rent
prices, forcing the working class out of cities and creating a massive crisis of urban
homelessness. We will draft and fiercely litigate for strict national rent-control laws.
Takweyat will legally cap annual rent increases, heavily tax unoccupied speculative
properties, and make it a criminal offense for a landlord to evict a tenant arbitrarily without
severe, legally proven cause.
27.3. The Legal Right to Safe, Accessible Public Transportation
A city without functional public transport traps the poor in geographic poverty, denying
them access to employment, education, and healthcare. We will file constitutional
petitions arguing that safe, subsidized public transit is a fundamental civic right. Takweyat
will sue municipal governments for neglecting transit infrastructure, legally forcing them
to reallocate budgets from elite highway projects into the development of expansive,
zero-emission mass transit networks.
27.4. De-privatizing Water and Urban Sanitation
Corporations are increasingly privatizing municipal water supplies, hiking prices, and
shutting off access to impoverished neighborhoods, treating a fundamental human need
as a luxury commodity. We will file massive public interest litigations challenging the
legality of water privatization. We will legally force the state to reclaim complete control of
urban sanitation and water distribution, ensuring free, clean drinking water is
constitutionally guaranteed to every citizen.
27.5. Disability Access as an Absolute Urban Right
Cities are built exclusively for the able-bodied, leaving individuals with physical disabilities
completely excluded from public buildings, sidewalks, and transit systems. We will launch
devastating civil rights lawsuits against municipalities and private developers that ignore
accessibility codes. Takweyat will legally mandate the immediate retrofitting of all public
spaces with ramps, elevators, and tactile paving, imposing daily, compounding fines until
compliance is achieved.

27.6. Reclaiming Public Spaces from Private Encroachment
The wealthy elite and commercial corporations constantly encroach upon public parks,
sidewalks, and coastal fronts, illegally annexing public land for private parking or
exclusive clubs. We will establish a legal watchdog to monitor urban encroachment.
Takweyat will bypass corrupt city inspectors, filing direct supreme court petitions to
dispatch state bulldozers to tear down illegal elite structures and permanently return the
land to the public domain.
27.7. Banning Hostile Architecture and Anti-Homeless Designs
City planners deliberately install spikes on flat surfaces, segmented benches, and
freezing water sprinklers to punish the homeless and prevent them from resting in public
spaces. We will legally challenge the use of this cruel, hostile architecture. Takweyat will
sue municipalities for violating basic human dignity, legally forcing the removal of these
structures and mandating the funding of safe, humane urban shelters instead.
27.8. Combating Urban Heat Islands and Green Inequality
Wealthy neighborhoods enjoy lush, state-maintained tree canopies, while impoverished,
high-density areas are left as concrete heat islands, leading to skyrocketing temperatures
and fatal heatstrokes. We will file environmental justice lawsuits against city planners for
this deadly disparity. We will legally compel the state to initiate massive, mandatory
afforestation projects exclusively in low-income urban sectors to ensure equitable climate
resilience.
27.9. Democratizing Urban Planning Committees
Urban development is currently dictated entirely by billionaire developers, corrupt
politicians, and isolated bureaucrats who design cities for the wealthy while ignoring the
working class. Takweyat will draft legislation mandating extreme transparency in city
planning. We will legally require the inclusion of labor union leaders, environmental
scientists, and slum representatives on all municipal zoning boards, granting them
absolute veto power over elite mega-projects.
27.10. Restitution for Preventable Infrastructure Failures
When a citizen is electrocuted by exposed wires during a monsoon or dies falling into an
uncovered manhole, the state treats it as a tragic accident rather than criminal
negligence. We will refuse to accept these deaths. Takweyat will file massive civil and

criminal suits against municipal authorities for gross infrastructural manslaughter, forcing
them to pay astronomical compensation that makes ignoring basic civic maintenance
financially ruinous.
28. Dismantling Media Monopolies & Information Democracy
28.1. Breaking Up Cross-Ownership of Media Conglomerates
A handful of billionaire families control the nation's television networks, newspapers, and
digital platforms simultaneously, ensuring only corporate-approved narratives reach the
public. Takweyat will lead massive anti-trust litigations aimed at shattering these media
monopolies. We will legally argue that cross-ownership destroys democratic discourse,
forcing the divestment of these conglomerates to ensure a fiercely competitive, diverse,
and independent press landscape.
28.2. Defunding State-Sponsored Disinformation and Propaganda
Governments utilize taxpayer money to fund massive advertising campaigns on compliant
media networks, effectively bribing the press to ignore state corruption and publish
propaganda. We will file constitutional challenges against the misuse of public funds for
political marketing. Takweyat will legally mandate the creation of an independent, judicial
oversight committee to strictly control and severely limit all government advertising
expenditures.
28.3. Banning Corporate Lobbying in Newsrooms
Fossil fuel companies, pharmaceutical giants, and real estate mafias frequently sponsor
news segments or secretly dictate editorial policies to ensure their corporate crimes are
never investigated. We will draft strict legislation criminalizing corporate interference in
journalism. We will legally compel all media outlets to prominently disclose their corporate
sponsors and board members on-screen during every broadcast, exposing inherent
biases to the public.
28.4. Protecting Independent Citizen Journalism and Whistleblowers
The most crucial evidence of state violence or corporate theft often comes from the
smartphones of ordinary citizens who are then targeted by cyber-crime laws. Takweyat
will provide an impenetrable, permanent legal shield for citizen journalists. We will legally
defend their right to record law enforcement and state officials in public, prosecuting any
police officer who illegally confiscates or destroys a citizenʼs recording device.

28.5. Enforcing Algorithmic Neutrality in Search Engines
Global tech giants manipulate search algorithms to bury independent journalism,
shadow-ban human rights activists, and promote state-approved narratives. We will
legally demand the complete transparency and auditing of these algorithms. Takweyat will
file civil rights lawsuits demanding algorithmic neutrality, legally forcing search engines to
prioritize objective, factual reporting over sensationalist, corporate-funded clickbait.
28.6. Criminalizing the Gagging of Whistleblowers by Media
Major media networks frequently acquire explosive evidence of elite corruption, only to
bury the story after backroom deals are made, betraying the whistleblowers who risked
their lives. We will establish legal precedents treating this suppression as an act of
accessory to the crime. Takweyat will sue media executives who deliberately kill
public-interest stories, legally forcing the publication of the suppressed evidence.
28.7. The Legal Right to Reply and Mandated Public Retractions
Media networks routinely destroy the reputations of innocent activists or marginalized
groups with defamatory broadcasts, later offering a tiny, unnoticeable retraction when
proven wrong. We will fiercely enforce the "Right to Reply." Takweyat will legally mandate
that if a network broadcasts a falsehood, the retraction and the victim's unedited reply
must be broadcast at the exact same prime-time slot, with the same duration and
prominence as the original lie.
28.8. Subsidizing Independent, Non-Profit Investigative Journalism
A democracy cannot survive without investigative journalism, yet independent reporters
are starved of funding and sued into bankruptcy by billionaires. We will legally advocate
for the creation of a massive, state-funded, but completely independently managed
"Journalism Endowment." This fund will be legally protected from state interference and
distributed exclusively to non-profit, investigative outlets dedicated to exposing high-level
systemic corruption.
28.9. Regulating Political Advertising on Digital Platforms
Political parties utilize foreign funding and hidden algorithms to micro-target vulnerable
populations with fake news, hate speech, and manipulated videos during elections.
Takweyat will draft pioneering digital legislation demanding absolute transparency. We will
legally force tech platforms to maintain a public, unalterable database of every political

ad, its funding source, and its targeted demographic, heavily fining platforms that fail to
police disinformation.
28.10. Global Alliance Against Imperialist Media Narratives
Western media conglomerates consistently frame the Global South through a lens of
inherent backwardness, justifying neo-colonial interventions and unfair trade policies.
Takweyat will partner with international legal and media coalitions to systematically
challenge these racist narratives. We will file defamation suits in international courts
against global networks that maliciously misrepresent our nations, forcing them to adopt
objective, de-colonized editorial standards.
29. Global Trade, Agricultural Tariffs & Economic Sovereignty
29.1. Defying Exploitative WTO Agricultural Mandates
Institutions like the WTO legally force developing nations to slash agricultural tariffs,
allowing heavily subsidized Western produce to flood local markets and bankrupt
indigenous farmers. Takweyat will provide top-tier legal counsel to the state to
aggressively challenge these exploitative treaties. We will draft domestic laws that legally
prioritize national food sovereignty over international trade agreements, erecting
protective barriers around our agricultural sector.
29.2. Suing for Bio-Piracy of Indigenous Crops and Medicines
Global pharmaceutical and agricultural conglomerates routinely patent indigenous seeds,
plants, and traditional medicinal formulas, stealing billions in potential revenue from the
Global South. We will establish a specialized intellectual property task force to challenge
these bio-piracy patents internationally. Takweyat will legally ensure that all patents based
on stolen indigenous knowledge are revoked, forcing corporations to pay massive,
retroactive royalties to the host nations.
29.3. Banning the Dumping of Subsidized Foreign Produce
Foreign superpowers deliberately dump excess, taxpayer-subsidized crops (like wheat
and powdered milk) into developing countries at prices below the cost of production to
destroy local agricultural independence. We will file immediate legal injunctions to halt
these imports. Takweyat will legally force the government to implement extreme
anti-dumping tariffs, prosecuting state officials who accept foreign bribes to allow these
market-destroying imports.

29.4. Rejecting Neo-Colonial Debt as a Trade Weapon
The IMF and World Bank use crippling national debt to legally force developing nations to
privatize their natural resources, slash education budgets, and surrender their economic
sovereignty. We will deploy international financial lawyers to utilize the legal doctrine of
"Odious Debt." Takweyat will argue in global tribunals that debts accrued by corrupt
dictators or through coercive economic diplomacy are legally invalid and must be
completely written off.
29.5. Halting the Privatization of Native Seed Banks
Corporate entities attempt to privatize and control national seed banks, legally forcing
farmers to purchase genetically modified, patented seeds every planting season. We will
launch aggressive constitutional challenges to protect our agricultural heritage. Takweyat
will legally classify native seeds and agricultural biodiversity as the inalienable property of
the public trust, making it a severe federal crime for any corporation to patent or
monopolize the national seed supply.
29.6. Mandating Fair Trade Margins for Global South Farmers
Multinational coffee, cocoa, and textile brands generate billions in profit while the farmers
in the Global South who harvest the raw materials live in extreme poverty. We will utilize
international trade law to fiercely litigate against these supply chains. Takweyat will legally
demand the enforcement of strict, minimum "Fair Trade" margins, ensuring that a just
percentage of the final retail price is transferred directly back to the original agricultural
worker.
29.7. Outlawing the Export of Crucial Fertilizers During Shortages
Corrupt cartels export locally produced, state-subsidized fertilizers to foreign markets for
higher profits, creating severe domestic shortages that cripple the national harvest. We
will push for anti-trust laws that treat the export of essential agricultural inputs during a
domestic crisis as an act of economic treason. We will legally force the state to
completely ban these exports, prosecuting the cartel leaders and seizing their assets to
compensate the farmers.
29.8. Establishing Regional Asian and African Economic Blocs
Developing nations are individually weak against Western economic hegemony, forced
into exploitative bilateral trade agreements. Takweyat will utilize its legal and policy
influence to advocate for the creation of massive, legally binding Asian and African

economic blocs. By pooling our legal resources and markets, we will negotiate with global
superpowers on equal footing, utilizing collective bargaining to end the era of
neo-colonial economic dictation.
29.9. Legal Sanctions Against Corporations Utilizing Child Labor
Multinational brands deliberately utilize complex networks of subcontractors to hide their
reliance on child labor and modern slavery in agricultural fields and sweatshops. We will
pierce the corporate veil through relentless international litigation. Takweyat will hold the
parent companies directly, criminally, and financially liable for abuses in their supply
chains, forcing them to implement total transparency and pay living wages at the very
bottom of the production line.
29.10. Ending the Hegemony of the US Dollar in Regional Trade
The forced reliance on the US Dollar for international trade allows foreign powers to
weaponize sanctions and manipulate local inflation rates, crushing the economies of
developing nations. We will provide the legal and policy frameworks required for the state
to bypass this hegemony. Takweyat will advocate for legally binding currency-swap
agreements and the development of regional digital currencies, ensuring total economic
sovereignty and immunity from foreign financial imperialism.
30. The Final Global Strategy: Transnational Resistance & Future Outlook
30.1. Establishing the Global Takweyat Legal Coalition
Injustice is transnational; therefore, our resistance must be global. We will formally
establish the Takweyat Global Legal Coalition, a decentralized network of the world's
most aggressive human rights lawyers, forensic auditors, and cyber-security experts.
This coalition will share intelligence, legal precedents, and resources instantly across
borders, ensuring that a multinational corporation escaping justice in one country is
immediately prosecuted by our network in another.
30.2. Weaponizing International Tribunals ICC, ICJ
We will stop begging for justice and start legally enforcing it on the world stage. Takweyat
will systematically escalate cases of state-sponsored genocide, corporate ecocide, and
historical colonial theft to the International Criminal Court ICC and the International Court
of Justice ICJ. We will utilize the absolute limits of international law to issue arrest
warrants for corrupt global leaders and corporate executives who have historically
operated with total impunity.

30.3. Decentralizing the Operational Command Structure
A centralized organization can be decapitated by assassinations, arrests, or state bans.
To ensure Takweyat is entirely indestructible, we will operate on a highly decentralized,
cell-based command structure. Regional chapters across Asia, Africa, and the West will
operate autonomously, bound only by the core ethical manifesto. If one operational center
is shut down by an authoritarian regime, three more will instantly rise to take its place.
30.4. Forging Alliances with Global Indigenous & Labor Movements
True power lies in the unified numbers of the working class and the marginalized.
Takweyat will formally ally its legal machinery with global indigenous land defenders,
massive labor unions, and climate justice movements. By providing an elite, impenetrable
legal shield to these grassroots movements, we will amplify their operational capacity,
turning localized protests into legally binding, systemic global overhauls.
30.5. Training the Next Generation of Legal Guerrillas
We are not just fighting the battles of today; we are arming the generation of tomorrow.
Takweyat will establish highly specialized, intensive legal boot camps for brilliant law
students from marginalized backgrounds. We will train them not as corporate bureaucrats,
but as "Legal Guerrillas"—experts in constitutional warfare, cross-examination, digital
forensics, and media manipulation, ensuring the survival and expansion of our aggressive
justice mandate.
30.6. Developing Independent Encrypted Communication Infrastructures
As states and tech monopolies increasingly monitor all digital communications, our
operational security is paramount. Takweyat will fund and deploy its own decentralized,
military-grade encrypted communication networks, completely independent of global
telecom monopolies. This will ensure that our whistleblowers, lawyers, and journalists can
coordinate transnational legal strikes with absolute anonymity and zero fear of state
interception.
30.7. Utilizing AI for Mass Legal Automation and Petitioning
To overwhelm corrupt legal systems, we will weaponize Artificial Intelligence. Takweyat
will develop proprietary AI legal software capable of drafting thousands of flawless,
customized habeas corpus petitions, Right to Information RTI requests, and civil rights
lawsuits in minutes. By automating the bureaucratic heavy lifting, we will flood negligent

state departments and corporate legal teams with an unstoppable tsunami of
accountability.
30.8. Financial Independence via Waqf and Global Crowdfunding
He who pays the piper calls the tune. To remain completely uncompromised, Takweyat
will absolutely reject funding from states, USAID, or corporate-backed NGOs. We will rely
exclusively on the permanent, self-sustaining Islamic model of Waqf (endowments) and
decentralized, anonymous micro-donations from the global working class. This
guarantees that our legal targets are dictated solely by the demands of justice, never by
the political agenda of a donor.
30.9. Continuous Adaptation and Policy Evolution
The tactics of oppression are constantly evolving through new technologies and legal
loopholes; therefore, Takweyat must evolve faster. We will establish an internal "Red
Team"—a group of brilliant strategists whose sole job is to ruthlessly critique our own
operations, find our weaknesses, and anticipate the next moves of state and corporate
adversaries. We will continuously rewrite our strategies, ensuring we remain the most
lethal and unpredictable legal force on earth.
30.10. The Ultimate Vision: An Era of Absolute Justice and Equity
Our ultimate goal is not to sustain a permanent war, but to win it. Takweyat exists to force
a paradigm shift in human history. We fight for a future where the rule of law is an
impenetrable shield for the weak and a terrifying sword for the corrupt. We envision an
era where education is free, the environment is sacred, and absolute justice is the
unshakeable foundation of a unified, equitable global society.
31. Animal Rights, Sentience & Welfare Legislation
31.1. Legal Recognition of Animal Sentience
The legal system treats animals as inanimate property rather than feeling, sentient beings
capable of suffering. We will draft and fiercely lobby for constitutional amendments that
legally recognize animal sentience. This will fundamentally shift the legal framework,
allowing our advocates to sue abusers not for "property damage," but for cruelty,
ensuring that the legal rights of animals to live free from unnecessary pain are strictly
codified.

31.2. Criminalizing Industrial Factory Farming
Factory farming subjects billions of animals to horrific, lifelong torture in battery cages
and gestation crates, entirely shielded from public view by corporate gag laws. Takweyat
will file massive public interest litigations against industrial meat and dairy monopolies.
We will legally force the abolition of extreme confinement practices, mandating severe,
unannounced veterinary audits and prosecuting corporations for systemic,
institutionalized animal cruelty.
31.3. Banning Cosmetic and Unnecessary Animal Testing
Corporations continue to burn, blind, and poison animals to test lipsticks, shampoos, and
household chemicals, despite the existence of superior, cruelty-free digital and biological
alternatives. We will draft legislation to completely ban the import and sale of any
cosmetic or household product tested on animals. We will sue corporations that falsely
label products as "cruelty-free," enforcing massive fines for consumer deception and
animal abuse.
31.4. The Eradication of Blood Sports and Animal Fighting
Dog fighting, bear-baiting, and unregulated racing are run by underground gambling
mafias with the tacit approval of corrupt local police. We will establish a specialized legal
task force to hunt down these syndicates. Takweyat will bypass local authorities, filing
direct supreme court petitions to dispatch federal raids, ensuring that the organizers,
breeders, and spectators of blood sports face severe prison sentences without the
possibility of bail.
31.5. Legal Protection for Stray and Urban Wildlife
Municipalities routinely respond to stray dog populations with brutal, illegal mass
poisoning or shooting campaigns, violating local and international welfare standards. We
will file immediate legal injunctions to halt these culls. We will legally compel city
governments to redirect those funds into humane, scientifically proven
Trap-Neuter-Vaccinate-Return TNVR programs, prosecuting municipal workers who
authorize the torture or poisoning of urban strays.
31.6. Regulating the Exotic Pet Trade and Captivity
The unregulated breeding and ownership of exotic animals (like lions and tigers) by the
elite is a dangerous status symbol fueled by the illegal wildlife trade. We will draft strict
laws completely banning the private ownership of exotic and endangered species.

Takweyat will legally force the confiscation of these animals from private estates,
relocating them to accredited sanctuaries at the former owner's expense.
31.7. Abolishing the Use of Working Animals in Extreme Conditions
Donkeys, horses, and mules are often worked to death in brick kilns and construction
sites under scorching heat, overloaded and beaten by desperate daily-wage laborers. We
will push for strict labor laws for working animals, mandating maximum load limits,
mandatory shade, and rest periods. We will establish free mobile veterinary clinics and
provide legal defense to rescue working animals that are visibly starving or injured.
31.8. Banning Live Animal Exports
Millions of live animals are crammed into highly unsanitary, boiling ships for weeks to be
exported for slaughter, resulting in massive mortality rates during transit. We will utilize
international trade and welfare laws to file injunctions against shipping companies
engaged in live exports. We will legally demand a transition to a "chilled meat only" export
model, preventing the prolonged, agonizing suffering of animals on the open ocean.
31.9. Establishing State-Funded Animal Rescue Infrastructure
Animal rescue is currently left entirely to underfunded private charities and volunteers,
while the state takes zero responsibility. We will file constitutional petitions arguing that
public health and ethics require state intervention. Takweyat will legally force
municipalities to establish well-funded, humane animal shelters and 24/7 rescue
ambulances, ensuring that injured or abandoned animals receive immediate,
state-sponsored medical care.
31.10. Integrating Animal Empathy into the Education System
Cruelty to animals in youth is a proven psychological precursor to violence against
humans, yet the education system entirely ignores empathy training. We will legally
compel national education boards to integrate animal welfare and empathy into the
mandatory primary school curriculum. By teaching children to respect all forms of life, we
will legally construct a future society that rejects violence at its very foundation.
32. Combating Global Militarism & The Arms Trade
32.1. Holding Arms Manufacturers Liable for War Crimes
Multinational weapons manufacturers sell billions in arms to authoritarian regimes and
paramilitaries, knowing explicitly that these weapons will be used to massacre civilians.

Takweyat will launch unprecedented international lawsuits against these defense
contractors. We will utilize the legal doctrine of complicity to charge corporate executives
as accessories to war crimes, demanding they pay massive restitutions to the civilian
survivors of the conflicts they armed.
32.2. Transparency in Defense Budgets and Military Spending
In many developing nations, the military consumes the vast majority of the national
budget, operating behind a veil of "national security" that prevents any civilian audit or
oversight. We will file sweeping constitutional challenges demanding the complete
declassification of non-strategic military spending. We will legally force the military to
submit to independent, civilian financial audits to eradicate massive internal corruption
and redirect excess funds to education and healthcare.
32.3. Banning the Use of Private Military Contractors Mercenaries)
Corporations and states increasingly utilize private mercenary groups (like
Blackwater/Wagner) who operate completely outside the Geneva Conventions, murdering
civilians with total legal immunity. We will campaign at the UN and the International
Criminal Court ICC to legally classify private military contractors as illegal combatants.
We will prosecute the corporate directors of these mercenary firms for transnational
murder and human rights atrocities.
32.4. Accountability for Drone Strikes and Extrajudicial Killings
Foreign superpowers routinely launch unmanned drone strikes in sovereign nations, killing
thousands of innocent civilians who are retroactively labeled as "collateral damage"
without a trial. We will provide elite legal counsel to the families of drone strike victims.
Takweyat will sue the foreign governments responsible in international courts, demanding
astronomical financial compensation and official apologies for these illegal, extrajudicial
executions.
32.5. Criminalizing the Deployment of Landmines and Cluster Munitions
Decades after wars end, unexploded landmines and cluster bombs continue to blow up
children and farmers in rural areas, rendering vast tracts of agricultural land unusable. We
will legally compel all nations to sign and strictly enforce the international treaties banning
these weapons. We will sue the states that planted them, forcing them to pay for massive,
immediate de-mining operations and lifetime medical care for the amputee victims.

32.6. Eradicating the Military Draft and Forced Conscription
Authoritarian regimes frequently force their youth into mandatory military service, utilizing
them as free labor or cannon fodder in unjust wars, brutally punishing those who refuse.
We will file international human rights petitions defending the absolute right to
"Conscientious Objection." Takweyat will legally protect citizens who refuse to kill for the
state, ensuring they are not imprisoned or stripped of their civil rights for their ethical
stances.
32.7. Prosecuting Sexual Violence as a Weapon of War
During armed conflicts, state militaries and rebel groups systematically utilize mass rape
and sexual violence as a calculated weapon to terrorize and destroy opposing
communities. We will deploy specialized war-crime tribunals to hunt down the military
commanders who authorize or ignore these atrocities. We will ensure that sexual violence
is prosecuted with the exact same severity as genocide, stripping the perpetrators of any
amnesty agreements.
32.8. Defending the Sovereignty of Demilitarized Zones
States routinely violate internationally recognized demilitarized zones and civilian safe
havens, bombing hospitals, schools, and refugee camps under the guise of hunting
insurgents. Takweyat will utilize international law to heavily sanction states that attack
these zones. We will file war crime charges against the commanding officers who
authorize strikes on civilian infrastructure, ensuring they are permanently banned from
international travel and their foreign assets are frozen.
32.9. The Legal Shield for Anti-War Activists
Citizens who protest against their own countryʼs involvement in unjust wars are frequently
arrested, labeled as traitors, and charged with sedition by the state. We will provide an
impenetrable, pro-bono legal defense for anti-war activists. We will systematically strike
down the treason laws used to silence them, legally establishing that opposing a
governmentʼs military aggression is the highest form of democratic patriotism.
32.10. Mandating the Repatriation of Prisoners of War POWs
Following border skirmishes or historical conflicts, nations often hold POWs in secret
facilities for decades, refusing to acknowledge their existence and torturing them for
information. We will file relentless habeas corpus petitions in international courts

demanding the immediate release of these forgotten prisoners. We will legally force
hostile states to allow the Red Cross unrestricted access to all military detention centers.
33. Global Vaccine Equity & Pandemic Preparedness
33.1. Abolishing Patents on Life-Saving Vaccines
During global pandemics, pharmaceutical monopolies prioritize massive profits over
human life, refusing to waive vaccine patents, which artificially restricts supply and
causes millions of preventable deaths in the Global South. Takweyat will launch an
aggressive international legal campaign at the WTO to permanently ban intellectual
property rights on essential, life-saving vaccines during declared global health
emergencies.
33.2. Suing for Vaccine Hoarding by Wealthy Nations
Western superpowers routinely pre-purchase and hoard billions of vaccine doses—far
more than their populations need—while frontline medical workers in developing nations
die unprotected. We will utilize international human rights law to sue hoarding nations. We
will legally demand the immediate, mandatory redistribution of excess medical supplies to
the Global South, establishing that hoarding during a crisis is a crime against humanity.
33.3. Transparency in State Medical Contracts
During health crises, governments bypass standard procurement laws, signing
multi-billion-dollar contracts with crony corporations for faulty ventilators, fake masks,
and substandard vaccines. We will file sweeping public interest litigations demanding the
immediate declassification of all emergency medical contracts. We will prosecute state
officials and corporate executives who use national health emergencies as a
smokescreen for massive financial embezzlement.
33.4. Protecting Frontline Medical Workersʼ Rights
Doctors and nurses are forced to work in highly infectious environments without adequate
Personal Protective Equipment PPE, hazard pay, or psychological support, resulting in
massive casualties among medical staff. We will file class-action lawsuits against the
state health ministries for gross negligence. We will legally compel the government to
provide guaranteed life insurance, hazard bonuses, and the highest grade of protective
gear to all frontline workers.

33.5. Combating State-Sponsored Medical Disinformation
During pandemics, populist politicians frequently promote fake cures, unproven remedies,
and conspiracy theories to distract from their own governance failures, directly resulting
in civilian deaths. Takweyat will legally challenge the immunity of these politicians. We will
file criminal negligence and manslaughter charges against state officials who intentionally
broadcast lethal medical disinformation to the public.
33.6. Legal Rights of Patients in Mandatory Quarantine
States utilize quarantine mandates to lock citizens in squalid, unsanitary government
facilities without basic nutrition, clean water, or access to their families, effectively turning
quarantines into prisons. We will file constitutional petitions establishing strict, humane
standards for mandatory isolation. We will sue the state for violating basic human rights,
legally forcing them to provide high-quality medical care and dignified living conditions in
all quarantine centers.
33.7. Eradicating Price Gouging on Essential Oxygen and Medicines
During medical crises, cartels hoard oxygen cylinders, antiviral drugs, and hospital beds,
selling them on the black market at extortionate prices while desperate patients suffocate.
We will push for severe anti-trust laws that categorize medical price gouging as
attempted murder. Takweyat will deploy legal teams to force immediate state raids on
hoarding facilities, ensuring the goods are confiscated and the profiteers face maximum
prison sentences.
33.8. Guaranteeing Sick Leave and Financial Support During Lockdowns
When governments declare sudden, nationwide lockdowns, daily-wage earners and the
working class are left to starve without any financial safety net, forcing them to break
quarantine to survive. We will legally force the state to guarantee universal basic income
and mandatory, fully paid sick leave for the entire duration of any state-mandated
lockdown. We will sue corporations that fire workers for adhering to quarantine orders.
33.9. Defending the Right to Refuse Experimental Treatments
In desperate situations, hospitals sometimes administer highly experimental or
unapproved drugs to marginalized patients without proper informed consent, using them
as non-consensual test subjects. We will establish a specialized legal watchdog to
monitor hospital treatment protocols. We will prosecute physicians who violate the

medical ethics of informed consent, ensuring that no patient is ever treated as a
laboratory animal.
33.10. Building Sovereign Biomanufacturing Infrastructure
The Global Southʼs reliance on Western pharmaceutical companies for basic vaccines is a
massive national security threat. Takweyat will file public interest litigations forcing the
state to invest heavily in domestic, state-owned biomanufacturing facilities. We will legally
mandate that a significant portion of the defense budget be permanently reallocated to
ensure the nation can independently research, develop, and manufacture its own
vaccines.
34. Wealth Redistribution, Tax Justice & Economic Equality
34.1. The Implementation of an Extreme Wealth Tax
A microscopic fraction of the elite holds more wealth than the bottom half of the entire
nation, hoarding resources while the masses starve. Takweyat will draft and fiercely lobby
for a radical "Extreme Wealth Tax" targeting billionaires, massive landholders, and
corporate dynasties. We will legally mandate that this tax revenue bypasses the general
bureaucracy and is deposited directly into a sovereign fund dedicated exclusively to free
public education and healthcare.
34.2. Eradicating Corporate Tax Havens and Offshore Evasion
Multinational corporations and corrupt politicians utilize a complex web of offshore shell
companies in the Cayman Islands, Panama, and Switzerland to evade billions in domestic
taxes. We will deploy international forensic lawyers to breach these financial fortresses.
We will file supreme court injunctions to instantly freeze the domestic assets, properties,
and corporate licenses of any individual or entity caught utilizing offshore tax havens.
34.3. Closing Loophole Subsidies for the Ultra-Wealthy
The government routinely grants massive tax exemptions, subsidized electricity, and free
land to elite industries (like sugar and real estate mafias) under the false pretense of
"economic stimulation." We will file massive constitutional challenges against these
discriminatory subsidies. We will legally force the state to immediately revoke these elite
privileges, demanding the retroactive repayment of decades of stolen public subsidies.
34.4. Regulating the Real Estate Black Market
Real estate is the primary vehicle for parking untaxed, illicit wealth, artificially driving

property prices so high that the middle class can no longer afford basic housing. We will
draft legislation legally mandating that all real estate transactions above a certain
threshold must be conducted digitally through heavily audited banking channels. We will
push for laws that allow the state to automatically confiscate any property purchased with
unexplainable, untaxed cash.
34.5. Heavy Taxation on Inherited Dynastic Wealth
Generational wealth creates a permanent, untouchable aristocracy that dominates the
economy without ever having to work, destroying the concept of a meritocracy. Takweyat
will legally advocate for aggressive inheritance taxes on massive estates. We will ensure
that the transfer of astronomical wealth is heavily taxed to fund national infrastructure,
preventing a handful of families from permanently monopolizing the nation's financial
resources.
34.6. Ending the Regressive Taxation System GST/VAT Abuse)
The state generates revenue primarily through indirect, regressive taxes (like VAT on food
and fuel), meaning a billionaire and a starving laborer pay the exact same tax on a loaf of
bread. We will file supreme court petitions arguing that regressive taxation is
fundamentally unconstitutional and discriminatory. We will legally force the government to
shift the tax burden directly onto corporate profits and luxury imports, zero-rating all
essential survival goods.
34.7. Full Financial Transparency of Elected Officials
Politicians enter public office with modest assets and leave a few years later as
multi-millionaires, hiding their wealth under the names of relatives and front-men. We will
legally mandate the absolute, real-time public disclosure of the financial assets of all
elected officials, their spouses, and their extended families. We will draft laws allowing for
the immediate impeachment and asset seizure of any official caught hiding their true net
worth.
34.8. Prosecuting the Facilitators Banks and Auditors)
Tax evasion on a massive scale is impossible without the active complicity of elite
auditing firms, corporate lawyers, and commercial banks who design the loopholes. We
will stop going just after the evaders and start targeting the architects. Takweyat will file
criminal conspiracy and fraud charges against the accounting firms and banking

executives who facilitate tax evasion, ensuring their operating licenses are permanently
revoked.
34.9. Universal Basic Income UBI as a Legal Dividend
The natural resources of a nation (oil, gas, minerals) belong to the citizens, yet the profits
are hoarded by the state and foreign corporations. We will file strategic litigations
proposing a "Sovereign Citizen Dividend." We will legally argue that a percentage of all
revenue generated from the extraction of national resources must be paid out directly to
every citizen as a Universal Basic Income, eradicating extreme poverty overnight.
34.10. Corporate Profit-Sharing Mandates
Workers generate the profits, but executives and shareholders extract all the wealth,
leading to massive income inequality and labor exploitation. We will draft radical
economic legislation legally capping the ratio between CEO compensation and the
lowest-paid worker's salary. Takweyat will aggressively push for laws that mandate
mandatory profit-sharing, ensuring that a legally defined percentage of all corporate
profits must be distributed directly to the employees who generated that wealth.
35. Civil Society Protection, NGO Independence & Activist Defense
35.1. Striking Down Draconian NGO Registration Laws
Authoritarian regimes weaponize bureaucratic registration laws to deregister, harass, and
shut down civil society organizations that critique state policies. Takweyat (تﯾوﻘﺗ) will file
massive constitutional challenges against laws that grant intelligence agencies arbitrary
veto power over NGO registrations. We will legally mandate that civil society operates
freely under standard corporate law, immune from politically motivated state interference
or sudden operational bans.
35.2. Defeating "Foreign Agent" and Treason Smear Campaigns
Governments routinely draft laws labeling human rights organizations as "foreign agents"
to delegitimize their funding and expose their staff to treason charges. We will provide an
impenetrable legal shield for civil society groups facing these smears. We will sue state
media and government spokespersons for defamation and political endangerment, legally
forcing them to publicly retract false espionage allegations made against humanitarian
workers.

35.3. Banning the Arbitrary Freezing of Charity Assets
During crackdowns, the state frequently instructs the central bank to instantly freeze the
bank accounts of human rights defenders and charities, crippling their ability to provide
aid or hire legal defense. We will file high-court petitions declaring extrajudicial asset
freezing entirely unconstitutional. Takweyat will legally force banks to immediately
unfreeze funds unless the state can provide concrete, court-verified evidence of financial
terrorism within 48 hours.
35.4. Legal Immunity for Disaster Relief Volunteers
Independent volunteers and civil society workers who rush into disaster zones or conflict
areas are often arrested by the military or police for bypassing corrupt state relief
channels. We will draft "Good Samaritan" and "Relief Immunity" laws. We will legally
establish that citizens engaged in saving lives during a declared emergency possess
absolute immunity from state trespassing or curfew violations, protecting the first
responders of the public.
35.5. Protecting the Right to Peaceful Assembly and Protest Camps
The state continuously utilizes anti-terrorism laws to violently dismantle peaceful protest
camps, arresting activists for "disrupting public order." Takweyat will continuously litigate
to protect the absolute constitutional right to assemble. We will file preemptive injunctions
preventing the police from using tear gas, water cannons, or baton charges on peaceful
crowds, prosecuting commanding officers who deploy disproportionate violence against
civil society.
35.6. Eradicating SLAPP Suits Against Activists and NGOs
Massive corporations utilize Strategic Lawsuits Against Public Participation SLAPP to
bury environmental and labor activists under millions of dollars in fabricated legal fees,
forcing them into silence. We will push for severe Anti-SLAPP legislation across all
jurisdictions. We will legally empower judges to immediately dismiss these malicious
corporate lawsuits, forcing the corporation to pay exorbitant punitive damages directly to
the targeted activists.
35.7. Defending the Anonymity of Donors to Human Rights Causes
Citizens who financially support anti-corruption or human rights organizations face
severe retaliation, tax audits, and harassment from the state. We will fiercely defend the
right to anonymous philanthropic giving. Takweyat will legally challenge state demands

for donor lists, utilizing advanced encryption and international trust laws to ensure that
the identities of everyday citizens funding the resistance remain permanently hidden from
vindictive state agencies.
35.8. Prosecuting the Surveillance of Civil Society Leaders
Intelligence agencies illegally wiretap, track, and physically stalk human rights lawyers,
journalists, and NGO directors to intimidate them and steal their data. We will file
high-level espionage and harassment lawsuits against the directors of these state
intelligence apparatuses. We will utilize independent digital forensics to prove state
hacking, legally demanding the immediate destruction of illegally acquired surveillance
files.
35.9. Securing Visas and Asylum for Persecuted Activists
When the state decides to permanently silence an activist, immediate extraction is the
only option. Takweyat will leverage its global legal network to secure emergency
humanitarian visas and political asylum for civil society leaders facing imminent
assassination or arbitrary life imprisonment. We will legally escort them across borders
and provide them with the infrastructure to continue their advocacy from secure,
international safe havens.
35.10. Mandating State Protection for Threatened Defenders
When an activist reports death threats from cartels or political mafias, the police routinely
ignore the complaint until the activist is murdered. We will establish legal precedents
making the state explicitly liable for the lives of human rights defenders. We will file
emergency petitions forcing the state to provide 24/7 armed protection to threatened
activists, holding the interior ministry criminally accountable if the activist is harmed.
36. Cybersecurity, Digital Forensics & Combating State Hacking
36.1. Prosecuting State-Sponsored Cyber Warfare Against Citizens
Governments deploy military-grade hacking divisions not against foreign enemies, but
against their own domestic journalists, lawyers, and political dissidents to steal
communications and plant fabricated evidence. Takweyat will file high-treason lawsuits
against intelligence chiefs authorizing domestic cyber-warfare. We will legally compel
internet service providers to block known state-sponsored malware signatures, protecting
the digital borders of the citizenry.

36.2. The Legal Inadmissibility of Hacked or Leaked Digital Evidence
State agencies routinely release illegally intercepted phone calls, private photos, and
hacked emails to the media to destroy the public reputations of political opponents or
activists. We will aggressively litigate to make all illegally obtained digital surveillance
completely inadmissible in any court of law. We will sue the media networks that
broadcast these illegal leaks, forcing them to pay massive damages for participating in
state blackmail.
36.3. Securing the Integrity of Electronic Voting Systems
Electronic voting machines and state election servers are highly vulnerable to digital
manipulation, allowing corrupt regimes to alter election results without leaving physical
evidence. We will file constitutional petitions demanding mandatory, open-source auditing
of all election software. Takweyat will legally enforce the implementation of voter-verified
paper audit trails VVPAT for every digital vote, ensuring absolute, tamper-proof
democratic transparency.
36.4. Defending the Legal Right to End-to-End Encryption
Authoritarian regimes are attempting to legally force tech companies to build "backdoors"
into platforms like WhatsApp and Signal, seeking the ability to monitor the private
conversations of billions. We will legally challenge any legislation that attempts to ban or
weaken encryption. We will argue that mathematical encryption is equivalent to the lock
on a physical home, and forcing a backdoor is an unconstitutional, mass digital break-in.
36.5. Accountability for National Database Breaches NADRA/Identity Systems)
Centralized national identity databases store the biometric, family, and financial data of
every citizen, yet they are frequently breached or sold by corrupt insiders to hackers and
foreign states. Takweyat will file massive class-action lawsuits against state database
authorities for gross negligence. We will legally force the state to decentralize this data
and pay heavy financial compensation to every citizen whose identity was compromised.
36.6. Eradicating Deepfakes as a Tool of State Blackmail
Generative AI deepfakes are being weaponized by state actors to create hyper-realistic,
fabricated videos of activists or politicians engaging in illicit acts, entirely destroying their
