leading to massive income inequality and labor exploitation. We will draft radical
economic legislation legally capping the ratio between CEO compensation and the
lowest-paid worker's salary. Takweyat will aggressively push for laws that mandate
mandatory profit-sharing, ensuring that a legally defined percentage of all corporate
profits must be distributed directly to the employees who generated that wealth.
35. Civil Society Protection, NGO Independence & Activist Defense
35.1. Striking Down Draconian NGO Registration Laws
Authoritarian regimes weaponize bureaucratic registration laws to deregister, harass, and
shut down civil society organizations that critique state policies. Takweyat will file
massive constitutional challenges against laws that grant intelligence agencies arbitrary
veto power over NGO registrations. We will legally mandate that civil society operates
freely under standard corporate law, immune from politically motivated state interference
or sudden operational bans.
35.2. Defeating "Foreign Agent" and Treason Smear Campaigns
Governments routinely draft laws labeling human rights organizations as "foreign agents"
to delegitimize their funding and expose their staff to treason charges. We will provide an
impenetrable legal shield for civil society groups facing these smears. We will sue state
media and government spokespersons for defamation and political endangerment, legally
forcing them to publicly retract false espionage allegations made against humanitarian
workers.
35.3. Banning the Arbitrary Freezing of Charity Assets
During crackdowns, the state frequently instructs the central bank to instantly freeze the
bank accounts of human rights defenders and charities, crippling their ability to provide
aid or hire legal defense. We will file high-court petitions declaring extrajudicial asset
freezing entirely unconstitutional. Takweyat will legally force banks to immediately
unfreeze funds unless the state can provide concrete, court-verified evidence of financial
terrorism within 48 hours.
35.4. Legal Immunity for Disaster Relief Volunteers
Independent volunteers and civil society workers who rush into disaster zones or conflict
areas are often arrested by the military or police for bypassing corrupt state relief
channels. We will draft "Good Samaritan" and "Relief Immunity" laws. We will legally
establish that citizens engaged in saving lives during a declared emergency possess

absolute immunity from state trespassing or curfew violations, protecting the first
responders of the public.
35.5. Protecting the Right to Peaceful Assembly and Protest Camps
The state continuously utilizes anti-terrorism laws to violently dismantle peaceful protest
camps, arresting activists for "disrupting public order." Takweyat will continuously litigate
to protect the absolute constitutional right to assemble. We will file preemptive injunctions
preventing the police from using tear gas, water cannons, or baton charges on peaceful
crowds, prosecuting commanding officers who deploy disproportionate violence against
civil society.
35.6. Eradicating SLAPP Suits Against Activists and NGOs
Massive corporations utilize Strategic Lawsuits Against Public Participation SLAPP to
bury environmental and labor activists under millions of dollars in fabricated legal fees,
forcing them into silence. We will push for severe Anti-SLAPP legislation across all
jurisdictions. We will legally empower judges to immediately dismiss these malicious
corporate lawsuits, forcing the corporation to pay exorbitant punitive damages directly to
the targeted activists.
35.7. Defending the Anonymity of Donors to Human Rights Causes
Citizens who financially support anti-corruption or human rights organizations face
severe retaliation, tax audits, and harassment from the state. We will fiercely defend the
right to anonymous philanthropic giving. Takweyat will legally challenge state demands
for donor lists, utilizing advanced encryption and international trust laws to ensure that
the identities of everyday citizens funding the resistance remain permanently hidden from
vindictive state agencies.
35.8. Prosecuting the Surveillance of Civil Society Leaders
Intelligence agencies illegally wiretap, track, and physically stalk human rights lawyers,
journalists, and NGO directors to intimidate them and steal their data. We will file
high-level espionage and harassment lawsuits against the directors of these state
intelligence apparatuses. We will utilize independent digital forensics to prove state
hacking, legally demanding the immediate destruction of illegally acquired surveillance
files.

35.9. Securing Visas and Asylum for Persecuted Activists
When the state decides to permanently silence an activist, immediate extraction is the

humanitarian visas and political asylum for civil society leaders facing imminent
assassination or arbitrary life imprisonment. We will legally escort them across borders

international safe havens.
35.10. Mandating State Protection for Threatened Defenders
When an activist reports death threats from cartels or political mafias, the police routinely

making the state explicitly liable for the lives of human rights defenders. We will file
emergency petitions forcing the state to provide 24/7 armed protection to threatened
activists, holding the interior ministry criminally accountable if the activist is harmed.
36. Cybersecurity, Digital Forensics & Combating State Hacking
36.1. Prosecuting State-Sponsored Cyber Warfare Against Citizens
Governments deploy military-grade hacking divisions not against foreign enemies, but
against their own domestic journalists, lawyers, and political dissidents to steal
communications and plant fabricated evidence. Takweyat will file high-treason lawsuits
against intelligence chiefs authorizing domestic cyber-warfare. We will legally compel
internet service providers to block known state-sponsored malware signatures, protecting
the digital borders of the citizenry.
36.2. The Legal Inadmissibility of Hacked or Leaked Digital Evidence
State agencies routinely release illegally intercepted phone calls, private photos, and
hacked emails to the media to destroy the public reputations of political opponents or
activists. We will aggressively litigate to make all illegally obtained digital surveillance
completely inadmissible in any court of law. We will sue the media networks that
broadcast these illegal leaks, forcing them to pay massive damages for participating in
state blackmail.
36.3. Securing the Integrity of Electronic Voting Systems
Electronic voting machines and state election servers are highly vulnerable to digital
manipulation, allowing corrupt regimes to alter election results without leaving physical
evidence. We will file constitutional petitions demanding mandatory, open-source auditing

of all election software. Takweyat will legally enforce the implementation of voter-verified
paper audit trails VVPAT for every digital vote, ensuring absolute, tamper-proof
democratic transparency.
36.4. Defending the Legal Right to End-to-End Encryption
Authoritarian regimes are attempting to legally force tech companies to build "backdoors"
into platforms like WhatsApp and Signal, seeking the ability to monitor the private
conversations of billions. We will legally challenge any legislation that attempts to ban or
weaken encryption. We will argue that mathematical encryption is equivalent to the lock
on a physical home, and forcing a backdoor is an unconstitutional, mass digital break-in.
36.5. Accountability for National Database Breaches NADRA/Identity Systems)
Centralized national identity databases store the biometric, family, and financial data of
every citizen, yet they are frequently breached or sold by corrupt insiders to hackers and
foreign states. Takweyat will file massive class-action lawsuits against state database
authorities for gross negligence. We will legally force the state to decentralize this data
and pay heavy financial compensation to every citizen whose identity was compromised.
36.6. Eradicating Deepfakes as a Tool of State Blackmail
Generative AI deepfakes are being weaponized by state actors to create hyper-realistic,
fabricated videos of activists or politicians engaging in illicit acts, entirely destroying their
credibility. We will collaborate with elite digital forensic labs to instantly debunk malicious
deepfakes. We will draft legislation making the creation of a political deepfake a severe
federal felony, equivalent to forging state documents or committing high treason.
36.7. Regulating the Sale of Exploit Zero-Days
Private cybersecurity firms and rogue hackers discover critical vulnerabilities (zero-days)
in popular software, selling them to authoritarian states for millions instead of reporting
them to the manufacturers. We will utilize international law to classify the unregulated sale
of zero-day exploits as international arms trafficking. We will sue the brokers of these
digital weapons when their exploits are used to harm human rights defenders.
36.8. Protection of Critical Infrastructure from Cyber Ransom
Hospitals, power grids, and municipal water supplies are increasingly targeted by
ransomware cartels, threatening thousands of lives when life-saving networks are shut
down. Takweyat will legally compel the state and private infrastructure monopolies to

upgrade their cybersecurity protocols to military standards. We will file gross negligence
lawsuits against hospital administrators whose failure to secure their servers resulted in
patient deaths.
36.9. Establishing Independent Cyber-Tribunals
Traditional judges lack the technical expertise to understand complex digital forensics,
algorithmic bias, or advanced cyber-espionage, leading to deeply flawed rulings in digital
rights cases. We will aggressively lobby for the creation of specialized, independent
Cyber-Tribunals. These courts will be staffed by a dual-panel of legal experts and elite
computer scientists, ensuring that judgments regarding digital rights are based on factual
technological realities.
36.10. Empowering Ethical Hackers and "White Hats"
Ethical hackers who discover and report massive security flaws in government portals or
banking apps are frequently arrested and charged with cyber-terrorism by embarrassed
state agencies. We will provide absolute legal protection for "White Hat" hackers.
Takweyat will draft "Safe Harbor" laws that legally immunize security researchers from
prosecution, forcing corporations to pay them massive bounties for securing the nation's
digital infrastructure.
37. Judicial Independence, Courtroom Democratization & Legal Reform
37.1. Dismantling Executive Interference in Judicial Appointments
The executive branch frequently manipulates the appointment of judges, ensuring that
only state-compliant, compromised individuals reach the Supreme Court to protect elite
interests. Takweyat will file sweeping constitutional challenges to strip the government of
its power to appoint judges. We will legally mandate the creation of a completely
independent, transparent judicial commission composed of civil society, senior bar
members, and academia.
37.2. Abolishing the Abuse of "Contempt of Court" Laws
Judges routinely weaponize archaic "Contempt of Court" laws to arrest journalists and
citizens who legitimately criticize corrupt judgments, effectively shielding the judiciary
from all public accountability. We will aggressively litigate to strike down broad contempt
laws. We will establish the legal precedent that while disrupting a courtroom is
punishable, analyzing and severely criticizing a written judicial verdict is a protected
democratic right.

37.3. Mandating the Public Livestreaming of All High-Court Proceedings
Justice must not only be done, it must be seen to be done. Behind closed doors, powerful
lawyers and corrupt state prosecutors manipulate proceedings out of the public eye.
Takweyat will file public interest litigations to legally mandate the live, unedited video
broadcasting of all High Court and Supreme Court hearings. This absolute transparency
will force judges and lawyers to act with impeccable integrity under the gaze of millions.
37.4. Breaking the Elite Monopoly on the Legal Profession
The legal profession is dominated by elite dynasties, with exorbitant bar association fees
and nepotistic networking shutting out brilliant, working-class law graduates. We will
legally challenge the exclusionary practices of national bar councils. Takweyat will
advocate for heavily subsidized bar exams, transparent merit-based chamber
placements, and the complete eradication of nepotism in the awarding of senior advocate
statuses.
37.5. Reforming the Corrupt System of Bench Allocation
Chief Justices often manipulate the roster, assigning politically sensitive cases to specific,
pliant judges to guarantee a ruling in favor of the state or military establishment. We will
file supreme court petitions demanding algorithmic, randomized bench allocations. We
will legally force the judiciary to eliminate human interference in case assignments,
ensuring that no chief justice can act as a gatekeeper for political masters.
37.6. Eradicating Procedural Delays and Adjournments
The judicial process is deliberately weaponized through endless adjournments, keeping
innocent people in jail for decades and bankrupting the poor through endless lawyer fees.
Takweyat will draft and strictly enforce procedural reforms. We will legally mandate strict,
non-negotiable time limits for the conclusion of civil and criminal trials, financially
penalizing judges and lawyers who intentionally stall cases for extortion or political
leverage.
37.7. Protecting Judges from State Blackmail and Coercion
Intelligence agencies frequently utilize illegal surveillance, blackmail regarding family
members, and physical threats to coerce judges into delivering state-mandated verdicts.
We will establish a highly secure, anonymous reporting mechanism for judges facing state
extortion. Takweyat will aggressively prosecute intelligence officials who threaten the

judiciary, providing threatened judges with elite, independent physical security details to
ensure fearless rulings.
37.8. Modernizing Archaic Colonial Evidence Laws
Many nations still rely on 19th-century colonial evidence acts that fail to adequately
recognize digital forensics, DNA, or satellite imagery, resulting in criminals walking free
due to technicalities. We will lead a comprehensive legislative overhaul of the Evidence
Act. We will legally integrate modern scientific and digital standards into courtroom
procedure, ensuring that justice is driven by irrefutable science rather than easily
manipulated witness testimonies.
37.9. Eliminating the Culture of VVIP Court Treatment
Wealthy politicians and billionaires are granted immediate, after-hours hearings and
instant bail, while impoverished citizens languish in jail for years waiting for a single
hearing date. Takweyat will file massive constitutional challenges against this blatant
violation of equal protection. We will legally force the courts to process all cases strictly in
the order they are filed, entirely blind to the wealth or political status of the plaintiff.
37.10. Democratizing the Language of the Courts
High courts operate exclusively in complex English, completely alienating the vast
majority of the population who cannot understand the proceedings determining their fate.
We will legally mandate that all court proceedings, verdicts, and legal documents must be
instantly translated and made available in Urdu and prominent regional languages. We will
break the linguistic barrier that keeps the common citizen intimidated and dependent on
elite lawyers.
38. Disaster Law, State Liability & Climate Emergency Accountability
38.1. Holding the State Criminally Liable for Flood Negligence
When monsoons hit, corrupt officials deliberately breach embankments to save the

mass murder and gross negligence charges against the irrigation ministers and

communities.

38.2. Embezzlement of International Disaster Relief Funds
During catastrophic earthquakes or floods, the international community donates billions,

financial ledgers for all disaster relief. Takweyat will prosecute state officials who siphon
off emergency aid, treating the theft of disaster funds as an act of high treason
punishable by life imprisonment.
38.3. Mandating Corporate Liability for Climate Disasters
The unprecedented heatwaves and super-floods destroying the Global South are the

fuel conglomerates. We will file international class-action lawsuits holding these specific
corporations financially liable for the damage. We will legally demand they pay into a

infrastructure in developing nations.
38.4. Enforcing Strict Climate-Resilient Building Codes
Corrupt contractors construct hospitals, schools, and bridges using substandard
materials, ensuring they instantly collapse during an earthquake or flood, resulting in

building codes. We will sue the contracting firms and state inspectors whose corrupt
approvals lead to structural failures, forcing them to personally fund the reconstruction of
the collapsed infrastructure.
38.5. State Obligations During Extreme Heatwaves
As global temperatures soar to lethal levels, the state continues to implement massive
power outages (load shedding) in poor neighborhoods, leading to thousands of deaths
from heatstroke. We will file supreme court petitions establishing that access to cooling
and electricity during a heatwave is a fundamental right to life. We will legally force the
state to halt all load shedding in residential areas when temperatures cross critical
thresholds.
38.6. Legal Protection for Climate Migrants Internally Displaced)
When rising sea levels or desertification destroys rural lands, millions migrate to urban
centers where they are treated as criminals, denied housing, and subjected to police
extortion. We will fiercely defend the rights of Internally Displaced Persons IDPs.

Takweyat will legally force municipalities to provide immediate, dignified housing and
employment integration programs, banning the bulldozing of migrant settlements without
permanent resettlement options.
38.7. Prosecuting the Timber Mafia for Landslide Causation
Illegal, mass deforestation by politically protected timber mafias destabilizes mountain
slopes, directly causing catastrophic landslides that bury entire villages alive. We will
refuse to label these events as "natural disasters." We will utilize satellite imagery to trace
the deforestation, filing mass manslaughter charges against the timber barons and the
corrupt forest guards who allowed the logging, holding them entirely responsible for the
deaths.
38.8. Mandatory State Disaster Insurance for the Vulnerable
Wealthy corporations have insurance to recover from disasters; the poor lose everything
they own and are pushed into generational debt. We will draft legislation legally
compelling the state to establish a "Universal Disaster Insurance Fund." Funded by heavy
taxes on the extractive industries, this fund will legally guarantee the immediate,
no-questions-asked replacement of homes, livestock, and crops for citizens living below
the poverty line.
38.9. Protecting Women and Children in Disaster Camps
During the chaos of a natural disaster, women and unaccompanied minors in state-run
relief camps face epidemic levels of sexual assault, human trafficking, and starvation.
Takweyat will establish a specialized legal task force to monitor emergency camps. We

prosecuting any state official or camp volunteer caught exploiting or abusing the
vulnerable displaced population.
38.10. Eradicating the Red Tape in Emergency Medical Responses
During massive emergencies, customs officials frequently delay the import of donated,
life-saving medical equipment and rescue helicopters to extract bribes from international
NGOs. We will file preemptive constitutional injunctions that automatically suspend all
bureaucratic customs protocols for medical and rescue imports during a declared national
emergency. We will prosecute officials who delay emergency aid for extortion as
accessories to mass murder.

39.10. Eradicating Nepotism in Entry-Level State Employment

41. Sub-National Autonomy & Devolution of Central Power
41.1. Dismantling the Centralized Authoritarian State
Central governments frequently hoard power, revenue, and resources in the capital,
treating provinces and states like internal colonies to be plundered. Takweyat will file
massive constitutional challenges to legally enforce the absolute devolution of power. We
will argue that centralizing authority violates the democratic right of local populations,
legally forcing the state to transfer political, financial, and administrative control directly to
local and municipal governments.
41.2. Retaining Provincial Wealth and Natural Resources
Resource-rich provinces are often kept intentionally impoverished while the central
government extracts their gas, oil, and minerals to enrich the capital elite. We will fiercely
litigate on behalf of marginalized provinces, utilizing constitutional mandates to challenge
federal extraction treaties. We will legally ensure that a minimum of 70% of all revenue
generated from natural resources remains permanently within the province of origin to
fund local education and healthcare.
41.3. Empowering Local Grassroots Governance
A true democracy operates from the bottom up, yet local governments are frequently
suspended or defunded by fearful federal politicians. Takweyat will establish a legal
watchdog to protect grassroots democracy. We will file immediate supreme court
injunctions against any federal attempt to dissolve local councils, legally mandating that
local governments have exclusive, constitutionally protected jurisdiction over their own
urban planning, policing, and municipal taxation.
41.4. Decentralization of the Federal Police Force
Federalized police forces often act as the private militias of the central ruling party,
deployed to crush regional dissent or enforce political hegemony. We will draft legislation

to entirely dismantle centralized policing in favor of community-controlled, localized
police forces. We will legally require that police chiefs are elected by or directly
accountable to the municipalities they serve, instantly prosecuting federal officers who
attempt to overstep local jurisdictions.
41.5. Linguistic Sovereignty in Regional Administration
The central state frequently imposes a single national language on legal and
administrative proceedings, effectively disenfranchising millions of citizens who speak
regional mother tongues. Takweyat will launch strategic litigations demanding absolute
linguistic equity. We will legally force the state to conduct all localized court proceedings,
public exams, and bureaucratic functions in the dominant regional language, alongside
the national language, preserving cultural autonomy.
41.6. Reforming Federal Tax Distribution Formulas
The federal formula for distributing tax revenue is often weaponized to punish states that
vote for opposition parties, starving them of development funds. We will file sweeping
public interest litigations challenging the legality of biased federal financial distributions.
We will legally compel the establishment of an independent, algorithmic, and transparent
financial commission that distributes tax revenue purely based on population need and
regional poverty indexes.
41.7. Veto Power Over Federal Mega-Projects
The central government routinely greenlights massive, environmentally destructive
infrastructure projects—like mega-dams or nuclear plants—within provinces that
vehemently oppose them. We will legally establish the absolute right of "Provincial Veto."
Takweyat will file constitutional petitions ensuring that no federal project can be initiated
without the explicit, legally verified consent of the local population and the regional
government affected by the project.
41.8. Abolishing Federal Overreach in Education
A single, centralized educational curriculum is often used as a tool for ideological
indoctrination, erasing the unique historical and cultural narratives of individual states. We
will fiercely defend the right of provinces to dictate their own educational frameworks. We
will sue the federal ministry of education for overreach, legally ensuring that history,
literature, and social sciences are taught from the authentic perspective of the region's
indigenous populations.

41.9. Establishing Regional Constitutional Courts
Currently, all final legal appeals must travel to a single, heavily politicized supreme court
in the capital, making ultimate justice inaccessible and highly expensive for the rural poor.
Takweyat will aggressively advocate for the creation of co-equal Regional Constitutional
Courts. We will draft legislation that permanently decentralizes supreme judicial authority,
allowing regional courts to issue final, binding verdicts on issues strictly concerning
provincial law.
41.10. Defense Against Federal Military Deployment in States
Central governments frequently bypass the constitution by deploying the military or
federal rangers into restive provinces to act as local law enforcement, leading to severe
human rights abuses. We will file high-treason lawsuits against federal executives who
order military deployments without explicit provincial requests. We will legally establish
that domestic military occupation of a state is an unconstitutional act of war against the
citizenry.
42. Energy Justice & The Right to Sovereign Power
42.1. Classifying Electricity as a Fundamental Human Right
In the modern era, denying a citizen electricity is equivalent to denying them water—it
destroys their ability to study, work, and survive extreme weather. Takweyat will file
unprecedented constitutional petitions establishing access to a baseline amount of
electricity as an indivisible human right. We will legally mandate that the state provides a
free, minimum lifeline quota of electricity to all households living below the poverty line.
42.2. Shattering Fossil-Fuel Power Cartels IPPs
Independent Power Producers IPPs and fossil-fuel cartels trap developing nations in
predatory contracts, forcing the state to pay them billions in "capacity charges" even
when no electricity is produced. We will launch an all-out legal assault on these corrupt
agreements. We will utilize forensic audits and international law to declare these contracts
inherently fraudulent, legally voiding the agreements and prosecuting the politicians who
signed them.
42.3. Democratizing Solar and Renewable Energy
The state often implements severe taxes on imported solar panels and bans net-metering
to protect the profits of the centralized, state-owned energy grid. Takweyat will fiercely

litigate against these anti-renewable policies. We will legally compel the government to
remove all taxes on solar technology and enforce laws that allow every citizen to
generate, use, and sell their own renewable energy back to the grid without bureaucratic
restriction.
42.4. Accountability for Toxic Coal Mining E.g., Thar Coal)
State-sponsored coal projects strip local populations of their land, poison their
groundwater, and cause massive spikes in respiratory diseases, all while the generated
power is exported to wealthy cities. We will file massive environmental and human rights
lawsuits on behalf of these communities. We will legally demand the immediate halt of
toxic extraction, forcing the energy companies to pay for the complete medical and
ecological rehabilitation of the region.
42.5. De-Privatization of National Power Grids
When national power grids are privatized, corporate entities immediately halt
infrastructure maintenance and exponentially hike tariffs to maximize shareholder profits,
leading to fatal urban blackouts. We will file supreme court petitions challenging the
legality of grid privatization. Takweyat will legally mandate the renationalization of energy
distribution, establishing that critical survival infrastructure cannot be operated as a
for-profit commercial enterprise.
42.6. Eradicating Circular Debt through Elite Recovery
The energy sector's "circular debt" is primarily caused by elite corporations, military
cantonments, and state bureaucracies refusing to pay their massive power bills, while the
deficit is recovered by overcharging the poor. We will establish a specialized legal task
force to audit these defaults. We will file injunctions to immediately disconnect the power
of elite defaulting institutions, seizing their assets to instantly liquidate the national energy
debt.
42.7. Legal Defense for Off-Grid Rural Communities
Millions of rural citizens exist completely off the national grid, ignored by the state
because laying infrastructure to their villages is not deemed "profitable." We will legally
challenge this discriminatory negligence. Takweyat will file public interest litigations
forcing the state to redirect elite energy subsidies into the immediate construction of
decentralized, solar-powered micro-grids for every marginalized, off-grid community in
the nation.

42.8. Suing for the Health Impacts of Urban Power Plants
Heavy-fuel oil and coal power plants located near dense urban centers emit deadly levels
of sulfur and particulate matter, drastically reducing the life expectancy of the
surrounding population. We will utilize independent epidemiological studies to launch
massive class-action lawsuits. We will legally force the state to permanently shut down
urban toxic plants, demanding billions in healthcare restitution for the citizens whose
lungs were destroyed.
42.9. Ending Monopoly Tactics in Gas Distribution
State gas companies deliberately restrict the supply of natural gas to residential areas
during winter to ensure uninterrupted supply to crony industrial sectors (like fertilizer and
textile cartels). Takweyat will legally intervene to reverse this prioritization. We will file
emergency high-court stays mandating that residential survival and domestic heating
legally supersede all industrial and commercial gas allocations during periods of extreme
shortage.
42.10. Advancing Sovereign Nuclear and Hydro Rights
Foreign superpowers frequently use geopolitical pressure to prevent developing nations
from advancing their sovereign, peaceful nuclear and hydro-electric energy capabilities,
seeking to keep them dependent on imported oil. We will provide top-tier legal counsel to
the state to navigate and defeat international treaties designed to cripple our energy
sovereignty. We will legally establish our absolute, non-negotiable right to pursue
complete energy independence.
43. Geriatric Justice & The Rights of the Elderly
43.1. The Legal Right to a Dignified Pension
Elderly citizens who spent decades building the nation are abandoned to absolute poverty
when they can no longer work, treated as expendable by both the state and corporations.
Takweyat will draft and fiercely litigate for a "Universal Elderly Pension Law." We will
legally mandate that the state provides an unconditional, inflation-adjusted living stipend
to every citizen over the age of 65, funded by aggressive taxation on corporate
monopolies.
43.2. Criminalizing Elder Abuse and Financial Exploitation
The elderly are highly vulnerable to extreme physical abuse, neglect, and financial

extortion, often perpetrated by their own children attempting to seize their properties or
bank accounts. We will establish a rapid-response legal desk dedicated exclusively to
elder protection. We will prosecute elder abuse with maximum severity, legally stripping
abusive heirs of their inheritance rights and freezing assets to protect the victim's
survival.
43.3. Free and Absolute Geriatric Healthcare
The medical industry views the elderly as highly profitable targets for expensive,
unnecessary interventions, while denying them basic, dignified palliative care. We will file
sweeping constitutional petitions to mandate entirely free, state-funded geriatric
healthcare. Takweyat will legally force public hospitals to establish specialized,
high-priority geriatric wards, suing medical institutions that deny life-saving treatments to
patients based on their advanced age.
43.4. Regulating the Senior Care and Nursing Home Industry
Unregulated private nursing homes frequently operate as abusive holding cells, where the
elderly are overmedicated, malnourished, and kept in unsanitary conditions to maximize
corporate profit. We will legally audit these facilities through unannounced legal raids. We
will sue negligent nursing homes for severe human rights violations, legally revoking their
operating licenses and prosecuting their administrators for gross criminal negligence.
43.5. Combating Age Discrimination in Employment
Seniors who wish or need to continue working are routinely fired or denied employment
by corporations that view them as liabilities, violating their fundamental right to earn a
livelihood. Takweyat will file massive civil rights lawsuits against companies that
demonstrate systemic ageism. We will strictly enforce age-discrimination laws, forcing
corporations to pay heavy punitive damages to older applicants and employees who were
illegally terminated.
43.6. Protection from Forced Evictions and Homelessness
Elderly tenants on fixed incomes are the first to be illegally evicted by landlords seeking to
gentrify properties and hike rents, resulting in a crisis of geriatric homelessness. We will
provide an impenetrable, pro-bono legal shield for elderly renters. We will draft legislation
that makes it absolutely illegal to evict any citizen over the age of 65 from their primary
residence without the state simultaneously providing identical, fully-funded alternative
housing.

43.7. Accessibility Rights in the Public Sphere
Cities, transport systems, and digital portals are designed exclusively for the young and
able-bodied, completely isolating the elderly from civic participation and basic mobility.
We will launch devastating lawsuits against municipalities for violating the accessibility
rights of senior citizens. Takweyat will legally mandate the retrofitting of all public spaces,
crosswalks, and government websites to ensure they are fully navigable by the elderly.
43.8. The Legal Right to Die with Dignity Palliative Care)
Terminally ill elderly patients are frequently subjected to painful, unwanted, and highly
expensive medical interventions that prolong suffering simply to generate hospital
revenue. We will draft and enforce "Living Will" and "Right to Dignity" legislation. We will
legally empower the elderly to explicitly refuse extraordinary medical interventions,
prosecuting doctors who ignore a patient's legal directives regarding their own end-of-life
care.
43.9. Protecting Seniors from Digital Fraud and Scams
The elderly are the primary targets of sophisticated cyber-criminals, phishing scams, and
fraudulent investment schemes, often losing their entire life savings in a matter of
minutes. Takweyat will hold the banking and telecommunications sectors legally liable for
failing to implement adequate, senior-specific security measures. We will legally compel
banks to instantly flag and halt unusual transfers from senior accounts, refunding stolen
money immediately.
43.10. State Investment in Multi-Generational Community Centers
The isolation of the elderly is a profound societal failure leading to severe depression and
cognitive decline. We will file public interest litigations forcing the state to fund the
creation of vibrant, multi-generational community centers. We will legally mandate that
urban planning includes integrated spaces where the elderly can safely interact, mentor
the youth, and remain active, respected participants in the cultural life of the nation.
44. Deep-Sea, Ocean & Planetary Commons Protection
44.1. Banning Deep-Sea Mining and Seabed Destruction
Multinational mining conglomerates are launching highly destructive operations to dredge
the deep ocean floor for rare minerals, threatening to permanently annihilate the most
fragile ecosystems on Earth. Takweyat will utilize international maritime law to file

immediate global injunctions against deep-sea mining. We will legally argue that the
seabed is the common heritage of mankind, pushing for a permanent, legally binding
global moratorium on its exploitation.
44.2. Prosecuting Illegal Industrial Whaling and Finning
Despite international bans, corporate fleets from superpowers continue to slaughter
highly intelligent whales and decimate shark populations for luxury consumption,
operating in international waters to avoid laws. We will establish a specialized
transnational legal team to hunt these fleets in global courts. We will sue the corporations
and the states that harbor them, demanding the confiscation of their vessels and severe
criminal charges for the executives.
44.3. Halting the Ocean Dumping of Nuclear and Toxic Waste
Developed nations and corporations secretly dump millions of tons of nuclear waste, toxic
chemicals, and raw sewage directly into the oceans, treating the planetary commons as a
free garbage disposal. Takweyat will collaborate with marine scientists to trace the origins
of toxic oceanic plumes. We will file massive environmental terrorism charges at the
International Criminal Court against the state entities authorizing the dumping of lethal
waste into the sea.
44.4. Defending the Sovereign Fishing Rights of Coastal Communities
Massive, foreign-owned industrial super-trawlers illegally invade the territorial waters of
developing nations, utilizing nets that scrape the ocean floor and completely destroy the
livelihoods of indigenous coastal fishermen. We will deploy legal teams to force the
national navy and coast guard to seize these illegal foreign vessels. We will legally
demand the permanent revocation of their fishing licenses and massive financial
restitution for local fishing communities.
44.5. Recognizing the Legal Rights of Cetaceans Dolphins/Whales)
Marine biologists have proven that cetaceans possess profound intelligence, culture, and
emotional depth, yet they are hunted or captured for brutal marine park entertainment.
Takweyat will file pioneering legal petitions to grant cetaceans "Non-Human Personhood"
status. We will legally demand the immediate closure of all commercial dolphinariums and
marine parks, forcing the corporations to fund the retirement of these animals to
open-ocean sanctuaries.

44.6. Combating Ocean Acidification and Coral Bleaching
The world's coral reefs, which support 25% of all marine life, are dying rapidly due to
ocean acidification caused directly by corporate carbon emissions. We will utilize
international environmental law to hold the top 100 global carbon-emitting corporations
legally liable for the destruction of the reefs. We will demand they pay into an
internationally managed "Oceanic Restoration Fund" to finance massive, global coral
regeneration projects.
44.7. Enforcing Strict Marine Protected Areas MPAs
Governments declare Marine Protected Areas on paper but allow commercial fishing and
oil drilling to continue within them due to corporate lobbying. Takweyat will fiercely litigate
to ensure these zones are absolute, "no-take" reserves. We will sue the state
environmental agencies for failing to enforce their own laws, legally mandating the
deployment of armed maritime patrols to permanently ban any extractive activities within
designated MPAs.
44.8. Eradicating Plastic Micro-Pollution at the Source
Microplastics have infiltrated the deepest ocean trenches and the human bloodstream,
driven by the unchecked production of synthetic textiles and single-use plastics. We will
file massive class-action lawsuits against the global petrochemical and fast-fashion
industries. We will legally force these corporations to drastically reduce plastic
production, holding them financially responsible for the unprecedented global health
crisis caused by their microscopic pollution.
44.9. Legal Action Against the Cruise Ship Industry
Mega-cruise ships are floating ecological disasters, dumping raw sewage, burning highly
toxic bunker fuel, and destroying fragile coastal ecosystems and port cities. Takweyat will
draft strict national legislation banning the entry of heavily polluting cruise ships into
sovereign waters. We will sue the cruise monopolies for massive environmental damages,
legally forcing them to transition to zero-emission technologies or face total operational
bans.
44.10. Protection of the Polar Regions Antarctica and the Arctic)
As the ice caps melt due to global warming, geopolitical superpowers and oil
conglomerates are aggressively mobilizing to drill for oil and militarize the Arctic and
Antarctic. We will form an international legal coalition to fiercely defend the Antarctic

Treaty System. We will launch preemptive lawsuits in global tribunals to permanently ban
any commercial extraction or military presence in the polar regions, preserving them as
demilitarized planetary sanctuaries.
45. Eradicating Transnational Human Trafficking Syndicates
45.1. Classifying Human Trafficking as a Crime Against Humanity
The global trafficking of women, children, and labor is a multi-billion-dollar enterprise that
operates with the complicity of corrupt border guards and politicians. Takweyat will
launch an international legal campaign to prosecute transnational human trafficking
exclusively under the jurisdiction of the International Criminal Court ICC. By elevating
the charge to a crime against humanity, we will ensure that traffickers can be hunted and
prosecuted by any nation on earth.
45.2. Piercing the Financial Networks of Trafficking Cartels
Human traffickers rely on the formal banking system and international money-laundering
hubs to clean their blood money. We will deploy an elite team of forensic accountants to
trace these illicit financial flows. Takweyat will file massive lawsuits against the
commercial banks and shell companies that knowingly facilitate human trafficking, legally
forcing the total seizure of cartel assets and the revocation of the complicit banks'
international charters.
45.3. Prosecuting the Demand: The Exploitation of Trafficked Labor
While smugglers are occasionally caught, the wealthy corporations, agricultural
monopolies, and elite households that purchase and exploit trafficked labor almost always
escape justice. We will legally shift the focus to prosecute the demand. We will file severe
criminal charges against the corporate executives and wealthy individuals who utilize
forced labor, ensuring they face the exact same prison sentences as the smugglers who
supplied the victims.
45.4. Decriminalizing the Victims of Human Trafficking
Law enforcement routinely arrests trafficked individuals for immigration violations,
prostitution, or forced petty theft, treating the victims as criminals while the traffickers
escape. Takweyat will provide immediate, absolute legal protection for victims. We will
systematically strike down laws that criminalize trafficked persons, legally forcing the
state to provide them with trauma care, absolute immunity, and secure visas, recognizing
them exclusively as victims of severe crimes.

45.5. Dismantling Corrupt Border and Immigration Agencies
Human trafficking syndicates cannot operate across international borders without paying
massive bribes to customs, police, and immigration officials. We will establish a
specialized anti-corruption task force targeting border enforcement. Takweyat will bypass
compromised internal inquiries, filing direct high-treason and trafficking charges against
corrupt state officials, ensuring that state complicity in modern slavery is punished by life
imprisonment.
45.6. Accountability for Online Platforms Facilitating Trafficking
Social media platforms, encrypted apps, and classified websites are heavily utilized by
traffickers to recruit victims and sell humans, while tech companies claim they are merely
"neutral platforms." We will file devastating civil and criminal lawsuits against these tech
giants. Takweyat will legally strip their immunity, holding tech executives personally liable
for failing to implement aggressive, AI-driven protocols to instantly detect and eradicate
trafficking networks on their platforms.
45.7. Global Extraction and Repatriation Protocols
Victims of trafficking are often trapped in hostile foreign countries, unable to speak the
language and abandoned by their own embassies. We will establish a rapid-response,
transnational legal extraction team. We will utilize international diplomatic law and habeas
corpus petitions in foreign courts to legally force host nations to locate, rescue, and safely
repatriate victims, bypassing the bureaucratic apathy of traditional consular services.
45.8. Confiscating Trafficker Assets for Victim Restitution
Victims of trafficking suffer unimaginable physical and psychological trauma, requiring
lifetime medical and psychiatric care, yet they are rarely compensated by the state. We
will draft legislation legally mandating that 100% of the assets seized from trafficking
cartels—including luxury real estate, vehicles, and bank accounts—must be liquidated
and placed into a secure trust fund dedicated exclusively to providing massive financial
restitution to the victims.
45.9. Enforcing Strict Oversight of Overseas Recruitment Agencies
Legitimate-seeming recruitment agencies frequently act as fronts for traffickers, luring
desperate workers abroad with false promises before confiscating their passports and
selling them into slavery. Takweyat will aggressively audit the international recruitment

industry. We will sue fraudulent agencies for human trafficking, legally forcing the state to
implement a centralized, heavily monitored digital registry for all overseas employment,
holding agencies strictly liable for the safety of the workers they place.
45.10. Eradicating Organ Trafficking and Exploitative Medical Tourism
A highly lucrative black market exists where the organs of impoverished individuals,
refugees, and prisoners are harvested and sold to wealthy medical tourists. We will
launch an all-out legal assault on the hospitals, surgeons, and brokers facilitating the
organ trade. We will prosecute organ trafficking as an act of extreme physical mutilation
and murder, pushing for international laws that make it a severe crime for citizens to travel
abroad to purchase illicit organs.
46. The De-Commercialization of Arts, Culture & Historical Heritage
46.1. Banning the Privatization of Historical Landmarks
Governments routinely lease ancient forts, historical palaces, and sacred heritage sites to
elite corporate conglomerates to be turned into exclusive luxury hotels, barring the public
from their own history. Takweyat will file massive constitutional challenges against the
commercial privatization of heritage sites. We will legally mandate that all historical
landmarks belong to the public trust in perpetuity, forcing the immediate cancellation of
corporate leases and ensuring free public access.
46.2. Protection of Indigenous Artisans and Cultural Intellectual Property
Multinational fast-fashion brands and global design houses consistently steal traditional
patterns, embroidery, and cultural designs from indigenous artisans, mass-producing
them for billions while the original creators remain in poverty. We will deploy intellectual
property lawyers to fiercely sue these global brands for cultural appropriation. Takweyat
will legally force corporations to pay massive royalties directly to the artisan communities
they plagiarized.
46.3. The Legal Right to Public Art and Uncensored Expression
Authoritarian regimes and conservative municipal boards frequently whitewash political
murals, ban controversial theater, and censor literature that challenges state narratives.
We will provide an impenetrable legal shield for artists and writers. We will file civil rights
lawsuits against the state for cultural censorship, legally establishing that public art and
political satire are absolute, constitutionally protected forms of democratic free speech.

46.4. Subsidizing the Humanities and Countering STEM Monopolies
The global education system is aggressively defunding literature, philosophy, and history
to focus exclusively on creating compliant workers for the tech and engineering
industries. Takweyat will file public interest litigations arguing that a society without the
humanities descends into fascism. We will legally force the state to allocate equal funding
and scholarships to the arts and humanities, ensuring the survival of critical thought and
cultural philosophy.
46.5. Repatriation of Looted Antiquities and Colonial Museum Collections
The greatest historical treasures of the Global South—from the Koh-i-Noor to the Benin
Bronzes—remain locked in Western museums, stolen during brutal colonial conquests.
We will not merely ask for them back; we will aggressively litigate. Takweyat will file
lawsuits in international courts and the home jurisdictions of these museums, utilizing
international property law to legally compel the immediate, unconditional repatriation of all
looted cultural artifacts.
46.6. Eradicating Corporate Sponsorship Monopolies in the Arts
Major cultural festivals, literary events, and national art exhibitions are entirely funded by
fossil fuel, banking, and real estate monopolies, allowing corporations to dictate the
cultural narrative and whitewash their crimes. We will draft legislation heavily taxing these
corporate marketing budgets. We will legally demand the creation of an independent,
state-funded "Arts Endowment" to ensure that artists can produce work entirely free from
corporate influence or censorship.
46.7. Protection of Regional Languages and Oral Histories
The centralization of the state often leads to the aggressive erasure of minority regional
languages, folklore, and oral traditions, which are replaced by a single national language.
Takweyat will file constitutional petitions demanding the state legally recognize and
actively preserve all indigenous languages. We will legally force the government to fund
massive digitization projects, recording and preserving the oral histories of elders before
they are permanently lost.
46.8. Banning the Destruction of Heritage for Urban Infrastructure
City planners and real estate mafias frequently bulldoze centuries-old neighborhoods,
shrines, and historical architecture to build modern highways and commercial plazas. We
will establish a legal watchdog to monitor all urban development near heritage zones.

Takweyat will file immediate, high-court injunctions to halt the demolition of any
historically significant structure, prosecuting the state engineers who authorize the
destruction of the nation's architectural memory.
46.9. Fair Compensation for Digital and Independent Musicians
Digital streaming monopolies (like Spotify and Apple Music) pay artists fractions of a
penny per stream, making it impossible for independent musicians to survive, while the
corporate executives extract billions. We will launch class-action anti-trust lawsuits
against global streaming platforms. Takweyat will legally push for the enforcement of an
"Equitable Remuneration Law," mandating that platforms pay a strictly regulated, living
wage per stream directly to the creators.
46.10. Establishing Independent Cultural Tribunals
Disputes regarding the authenticity of artifacts, the allocation of state arts funding, and
the censorship of literature are currently handled by uneducated bureaucrats or
compromised civil courts. We will vigorously advocate for the creation of Independent
Cultural Tribunals. Staffed by legal experts, respected historians, and veteran artists,
these tribunals will have the absolute authority to protect the nation's cultural heritage
from state interference and corporate exploitation.
47. Genetic Privacy, Bioethics & Protection Against Eugenics
47.1. Banning the Corporate Patenting of Human DNA
Biotech and pharmaceutical corporations are aggressively mapping the human genome,
attempting to legally patent specific human genes to monopolize treatments for cancer
and genetic diseases. Takweyat will file sweeping international lawsuits to permanently
ban the patenting of human biology. We will legally establish that the human genetic code
is the sacred, un-ownable heritage of humanity, prosecuting any corporation attempting
to claim ownership over human DNA.
47.2. Strict Prohibition of Genetic Discrimination
Insurance companies and employers are beginning to utilize covert genetic testing and
family medical histories to deny healthcare coverage or employment to individuals
predisposed to certain diseases. We will draft impenetrable "Genetic Non-Discrimination"
legislation. Takweyat will sue insurance conglomerates that utilize genetic data to assess
risk, legally making it a severe civil rights violation to discriminate against any citizen
based on their biological makeup.

47.3. Criminalizing Unauthorized Biometric and DNA Harvesting
State police and private intelligence firms routinely harvest the DNA of citizens upon
arrest for minor crimes, building massive, unregulated genetic databases without
warrants or consent. We will file high-court petitions to legally force the destruction of
these illegal genetic databases. We will legally mandate that DNA can only be collected
with a direct, specific judicial warrant for a severe felony, prosecuting officials who
engage in mass biological surveillance.
47.4. Absolute Moratorium on Human Germline Editing CRISPR
The unchecked advancement of genetic editing technologies (like CRISPR threatens to
create a dystopian future where the wealthy can engineer "designer babies" with
enhanced intelligence and physical traits, cementing inequality into the human species
itself. Takweyat will lead a global legal coalition demanding an absolute, legally binding
international moratorium on human germline modification. We will push for severe
criminal penalties for scientists who attempt unauthorized human genetic engineering.
47.5. Regulating the Commercial DNA Ancestry Industry
Corporations like 23andMe market "fun" ancestry tests to the public, only to secretly sell
the highly sensitive genetic data of millions of users to law enforcement and
pharmaceutical giants. We will launch massive class-action lawsuits against commercial
DNA testing companies for privacy fraud. Takweyat will legally force these companies to
obtain explicit, secondary consent before sharing any data, and demand they delete all
stored genetic profiles upon user request.
47.6. Protecting Indigenous Populations from Genetic Exploitation
Biotech companies frequently target isolated indigenous tribes, harvesting their unique
DNA to research disease resistance, entirely without providing the tribes any medical or
financial benefit. We will deploy bioethics lawyers to fiercely defend indigenous genetic
sovereignty. We will sue research institutions for biological colonialism, legally forcing
them to destroy illegally obtained samples and pay massive royalties to the tribes for any
medical breakthroughs derived from their DNA.
47.7. Banning State-Mandated Sterilization and Reproductive Coercion
Historically and currently, authoritarian states utilize forced sterilization, coerced
contraception, and punitive reproductive laws to control the populations of minority and

impoverished communities, a practice rooted in eugenics. Takweyat will prosecute forced
sterilization as a severe crime against humanity. We will file constitutional challenges
guaranteeing absolute bodily autonomy and reproductive freedom for all citizens,
irrespective of their race, religion, or economic status.
47.8. Transparency in Artificial Womb and Cloning Research
The development of artificial wombs and human cloning technologies operates in a highly
secretive, unregulated corporate sphere, raising profound ethical questions about the
commodification of human life. We will legally compel total transparency in all advanced
reproductive research. Takweyat will draft strict bioethical frameworks, mandating that
such research can only proceed under the direct supervision of independent, global
ethical tribunals, not profit-driven corporate boards.
47.9. Accountability for Environmental Mutagens Toxins Altering DNA
Multinational chemical and agricultural corporations routinely pollute marginalized
communities with toxic heavy metals and pesticides that are scientifically proven to cause
severe genetic mutations and birth defects in the local population. We will file devastating
lawsuits against these chemical giants for generational biological damage. We will legally
force them to pay for the lifetime specialized care of every child born with defects caused
by their toxic negligence.
47.10. Establishing a Global Bioethics Court
The rapid acceleration of biotechnology is outpacing national laws, leaving humanity
vulnerable to corporate experimentation. Takweyat will advocate at the UN for the
creation of a specialized Global Bioethics Court. This court will possess the ultimate
international legal authority to strictly regulate genetic engineering, prosecute bio-crimes,
and ensure that all advancements in human biology are deployed for universal healing,
not corporate profit or state eugenics.
48. Global Extradition & Abolishing Elite Safe Havens
48.1. Stripping Diplomatic Immunity for Severe Crimes
Corrupt politicians and oligarchs frequently purchase diplomatic passports from small
nations, utilizing "diplomatic immunity" to travel freely and evade prosecution for massive
financial crimes and human rights abuses. Takweyat will aggressively challenge the abuse
of the Vienna Convention in international courts. We will legally establish that diplomatic

immunity is immediately null and void in cases involving grand corruption, terrorism, or
crimes against humanity.
48.2. Dismantling the "Golden Visa" and Citizenship-by-Investment Industry
Wealthy criminals loot developing nations and immediately purchase citizenship in
Western countries (like the UK or Caribbean islands) through "Golden Visa" programs,
buying a permanent safe haven. We will file transnational lawsuits targeting the sovereign
states that sell these visas. Takweyat will legally compel these nations to revoke the
citizenship of convicted embezzlers and immediately extradite them back to their home
countries to face justice.
48.3. Closing Offshore Financial Fortresses Switzerland, Panama)
The global elite utilize the banking secrecy laws of offshore tax havens to hide the wealth
they have stolen from the working class, making it legally impossible for developing
nations to recover the funds. We will deploy an elite international legal coalition to shatter
these secrecy laws. We will utilize international anti-corruption treaties to force offshore
banks to open their ledgers, legally seizing and repatriating the stolen assets of the
corrupt elite.
48.4. Enforcing Interpol Red Notices Against the Elite
While Interpol aggressively hunts down ordinary criminals, political elites and billionaires
with Red Notices frequently travel the globe unhindered, protected by geopolitical
alliances. Takweyat will file legal actions against the law enforcement agencies of host
nations that refuse to execute valid Interpol warrants against the elite. We will legally
demand the immediate arrest and extradition of high-profile fugitives, regardless of their
wealth or political connections.
48.5. Prosecuting the Enablers Western Law Firms and Auditors)
Corrupt politicians cannot launder billions or secure safe havens without the active, highly
paid assistance of elite Western law firms, real estate agents, and auditing
conglomerates. We will shift the legal focus to prosecute these white-collar enablers.
Takweyat will file criminal conspiracy charges against the international firms that
knowingly facilitate money laundering and evade extradition, demanding they be heavily
fined and disbarred globally.

48.6. Seizing Luxury Assets in Foreign Jurisdictions
Corrupt officials from the Global South frequently invest their stolen wealth in luxury real
estate in London, Dubai, and New York, living in extreme opulence while their home
countries starve. We will utilize the "Unexplained Wealth Orders" UWOs and
international asset-recovery laws to target these properties. Takweyat will legally freeze
and auction off these foreign luxury assets, returning the proceeds directly to the national
treasury of the victimized state.
48.7. Banning Asylum for Corrupt Politicians
The international asylum system was designed to protect persecuted activists and
refugees, yet corrupt dictators and politicians routinely abuse it, claiming "political
persecution" to avoid facing trial for their crimes. We will file fierce legal interventions in
the asylum courts of host nations. Takweyat will present irrefutable evidence of their
financial crimes, legally ensuring their asylum applications are rejected and they are
immediately deported to face justice.
48.8. Establishing Fast-Track Anti-Corruption Extradition Treaties
Extradition processes currently take decades, bogged down in endless appeals funded by
the stolen wealth of the fugitive. We will draft and aggressively lobby for bilateral,
fast-track anti-corruption extradition treaties between developing nations and elite safe
havens. Takweyat will legally streamline the process, ensuring that once a grand
corruption indictment is finalized, the extradition of the elite fugitive is executed within
weeks, not years.
48.9. Creating a Global Public Registry of Elite Fugitives
The global public is often unaware of the massive crimes committed by the billionaires
and politicians living in their neighborhoods. Takweyat will establish and maintain a highly
publicized, undeniable digital registry of elite fugitives. We will utilize this platform to
launch relentless public and legal pressure campaigns against the governments harboring
them, making it politically toxic and legally perilous for any nation to act as a safe haven.
48.10. Prosecuting the Host Nations for Complicity
If a Western nation consistently harbors corrupt politicians and refuses to freeze their
stolen assets, that nation is complicit in the economic destruction of the Global South.
Takweyat will launch unprecedented legal action in international tribunals, suing the host
nations directly. We will legally demand that the harboring state pays massive financial

compensation to the developing nation equivalent to the total amount of wealth they
allowed the fugitive to launder.
49. Sovereign Technological Infrastructure De-Westernizing the Internet)
49.1. Breaking Reliance on Foreign Tech Monopolies Google, AWS, Meta)
The developing world relies entirely on Western technology conglomerates for its digital
infrastructure, email, cloud storage, and social media, granting foreign entities absolute
control over the nation's digital nervous system. Takweyat will file strategic litigations
forcing the state to break this reliance. We will legally compel the government to invest
heavily in building sovereign, state-backed, highly secure cloud infrastructure and
decentralized communication platforms.
49.2. Establishing Domestic Data Sovereignty Laws
Western tech giants harvest the biometric, financial, and behavioral data of billions of
citizens in the Global South, storing it on foreign servers where it is subject to the
surveillance laws of foreign intelligence agencies. We will draft uncompromising Data
Sovereignty legislation. Takweyat will legally force all multinational tech companies to
construct physical data centers within the host country, keeping citizen data strictly under
domestic legal jurisdiction.
49.3. Promoting Open-Source Software in State Institutions
Governments spend billions in taxpayer money purchasing proprietary software licenses
from foreign corporations (like Microsoft), trapping the state in a permanent, expensive
cycle of dependency. We will file public interest litigations to mandate the complete
transition of all government, military, and educational systems to Open-Source Software
OSS. This will ensure absolute digital security, massive financial savings, and
technological independence.
49.4. Banning the Import of Compromised Surveillance Hardware
Developing nations frequently purchase cheap telecommunications equipment, CCTV
networks, and surveillance hardware from foreign superpowers, hardware that is heavily
suspected of containing hidden "backdoors" for state espionage. Takweyat will legally
force the state to subject all imported technological infrastructure to brutal, independent
security audits. We will sue procurement officials who authorize the installation of
compromised foreign hardware.

49.5. Funding the Sovereign Development of Artificial Intelligence
The foundational models of Artificial Intelligence are currently controlled entirely by
Western and Eastern superpowers, trained on biased datasets that ignore or actively
marginalize the languages and cultures of the Global South. We will push for massive
state funding for the development of sovereign AI. Takweyat will legally ensure that the
nation builds its own AI models, trained on domestic languages, ethics, and legal
frameworks to prevent algorithmic colonialism.
49.6. Creating an Independent Global South Internet Backbone
The physical infrastructure of the internet—the undersea fiber-optic cables—is owned
and monitored primarily by Western telecom monopolies and intelligence agencies. We
will advocate for a geopolitical alliance of developing nations to fund and lay an
independent, highly encrypted network of undersea cables. Takweyat will provide the
legal framework for this joint venture, ensuring the Global South's internet traffic cannot
be intercepted or shut down by foreign powers.
49.7. Securing Domestic Supply Chains for Microchips
True technological sovereignty is impossible without the ability to manufacture
semiconductors and microchips, yet the global supply chain is entirely monopolized by a
few foreign nations. We will file constitutional petitions forcing the state to classify
semiconductor manufacturing as an issue of supreme national security. We will legally
mandate the reallocation of defense budgets to heavily subsidize the creation of a
domestic, high-tech microchip manufacturing industry.
49.8. Defending the Right to Cryptographic Sovereignty
Foreign powers consistently attempt to dictate global encryption standards, pushing weak
encryption algorithms that their own intelligence agencies can easily break. We will
deploy cybersecurity lawyers to fiercely defend our cryptographic independence.
Takweyat will legally mandate that the national digital infrastructure utilizes only the most
advanced, mathematically proven, open-source encryption protocols, entirely rejecting
compromised international standards.
49.9. Combating Digital Colonialism in ECommerce
Foreign e-commerce giants (like Amazon or Alibaba) frequently enter developing markets
and utilize predatory pricing to bankrupt local businesses, eventually monopolizing the
entire domestic retail sector. We will launch aggressive anti-trust lawsuits against these

foreign monopolies. Takweyat will draft strict legislation legally limiting their market share,
imposing heavy taxes on foreign platforms to heavily subsidize and protect local,
sovereign digital marketplaces.
49.10. Mandating Digital Literacy and Coding in Primary Education
A nation cannot achieve technological sovereignty if its population remains digitally
illiterate and dependent on foreign software. We will file public interest litigations
demanding the total overhaul of the national curriculum. Takweyat will legally force the
state to make advanced computer science, coding, and cybersecurity mandatory subjects
starting in primary school, ensuring the next generation is capable of building and
defending their own digital world.
50. The Takweyat Global Charter: The Constitution of Resistance
50.1. Absolute Commitment to the Rule of Law
The final pillar of the Takweyat Plan is our unshakeable commitment to the absolute
supremacy of the law. We declare that no entity—no general, no billionaire, no
multinational corporation, and no prime minister—is above the law. Takweyat will utilize
every legal mechanism available, from local magistrates to the International Criminal
Court, to ruthlessly hunt down and prosecute those who believe their wealth or power
grants them immunity from justice.
50.2. The Eradication of Systemic Fear
The primary weapon of the oppressor is fear—fear of state violence, fear of poverty, fear
of imprisonment. The Takweyat Global Charter is designed to completely eradicate this
fear from the marginalized. By providing an elite, impenetrable, and absolutely free legal
shield to every victim of injustice, we will empower the citizenry to stand up to corruption,
knowing that an army of the world's greatest legal minds stands fiercely behind them.
50.3. The Fusion of Islamic Ethics and Universal Human Rights
Takweyat rejects the false dichotomy between modern human rights and Islamic values.
Our charter is built upon the profound, revolutionary Islamic principles of absolute justice
Adl, societal balance Mizan, and the fierce defense of the oppressed Mustadhafin).
We will utilize this profound ethical framework to invigorate the modern legal system,
proving that true faith demands the relentless, uncompromising pursuit of global equity
and human dignity.

50.4. Total Financial and Ideological Independence
To fight the elite, we must never be reliant on them. The Takweyat Charter explicitly
forbids the acceptance of conditional funding from governments, corporate monopolies,
or foreign imperialist NGOs. We will operate exclusively through the decentralized power
of the working class and the sustainable model of the Waqf (endowment). This absolute
financial independence guarantees that our legal strikes will remain pure,
uncompromised, and utterly devastating.
50.5. The Democratization of Elite Legal Power
For centuries, elite legal representation was a weapon wielded exclusively by the wealthy
to crush the poor. Takweyat will reverse this paradigm entirely. We will draft the greatest
legal minds, the most brilliant forensic auditors, and the most aggressive constitutional
experts, and we will deploy them exclusively for the defense of the marginalized. We will
turn the law from a tool of oppression into an unstoppable engine of liberation.
50.6. Relentless Offensive Litigation Lawfare)
Takweyat will not merely play defense; we will launch an era of relentless offensive
lawfare. We will not wait for the poor to be evicted or the environment to be destroyed
before we act. We will proactively file thousands of preemptive public interest litigations,
constitutional challenges, and civil rights lawsuits, overwhelming the corrupt state
apparatus and forcing systemic reform through the sheer, crushing volume of our legal
attacks.
50.7. The Centrality of Education and Intellectual Liberation
The courtroom is where we fight the battles of today, but the classroom is where we win
the future. The Takweyat Charter holds that the establishment of our free, technologically
advanced, and ethically grounded educational network is equal in importance to our legal
victories. By educating the next generation to recognize propaganda and understand their
constitutional rights, we are immunizing the future against tyranny.
50.8. Solidarity with Global Resistance Movements
Injustice in one nation is a threat to justice everywhere. The Takweyat Charter formally
allies our movement with every legitimate struggle against oppression on Earth—from
indigenous land defenders in the Amazon to the victims of imperialist wars in the Middle
East. We will share our legal strategies, digital infrastructure, and resources across
borders, forging a unified, transnational resistance that is too massive to be defeated.

50.9. Unyielding Resilience and Generational Continuity
The forces we are fighting—corporate greed, state authoritarianism, and systemic
bigotry—have existed for millennia and will not be defeated overnight. The Takweyat
Charter demands unyielding resilience. When we lose a case, we will appeal; when a law
is unjust, we will strike it down; when an activist is arrested, we will free them. We will
train generations of successors, ensuring this movement outlives its founders and fights
until total victory is achieved.
50.10. The Ultimate Oath of Takweyat (تﯾوﻘﺗ)
We, the professionals, advocates, and volunteers of Takweyat, swear our ultimate
allegiance to the pursuit of truth and the defense of the vulnerable. We reject the corrupt
status quo. We will not rest, we will not retreat, and we will not compromise. We will utilize
the full, terrifying power of the law to shatter the chains of the oppressed, rebuild the
foundations of a just society, and ensure that the arc of history is violently bent toward
absolute justice.
